These quantities serve as inputs to a two-particle Hamiltonian that is constructed from single-particle Slater determinants through the configuration interaction (CI) method. By diagonalizing this two-particle Hamiltonian, the eigenstates and eigenenergies of the DQD system are obtained, together with their exchange coupling. We show that our simulation framework, using a reduced number of basis states, is capable of reproducing the magneto-electrostatic behavior of the devices of interest, as predicted from theory and observed experimentally. We finally leverage our approach to determine the optimal operating conditions of a two-qubit quantum logic gate implemented in a Si FinFET structure.

</details>


### [108] [Optimizing and Certifying Multipartite Permutationally Invariant Bell Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08462)
*Jin-Fu Chen,Mengyao Hu,Jordi Tura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的方法来构造和认证置换不变的贝尔不等式，仅使用单体和两体关联函数。该方法在大量参与方和大测量数下获得鲁棒的量子违背，并证明无限N下的比值收敛到简单有理数及coth(1)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体贝尔非局域性表征受限于经典和量子优化问题的维度爆炸，现有方法难以扩展到大量粒子数和大测量设置。

Method: 利用置换不变性，仅基于单体和两体关联函数构造贝尔不等式族；通过优化量子值与经典界限的比值增强抗噪声鲁棒性；使用半定规划认证量子违背；分析无限N极限下的渐近行为。

Result: 在无限N极限下，对于有限测量数m，比值取简单有理数；当m→∞时比值收敛到coth(1)。优化后的不等式能通过集体测量高效检测多体贝尔非局域性，更多测量设置导致更强的违背。

Conclusion: 该可扩展方法有效克服了多体贝尔非局域性表征中的维数灾难，为大规模量子关联的设备无关探测提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Multipartite Bell nonlocality provides a device-independent probe of many-body quantum correlations, but its characterization is limited by the rapid growth of the underlying classical and quantum optimization problems. We develop a scalable method for constructing and certifying permutationally invariant Bell inequalities using only one- and two-body correlators. The construction gives families of inequalities with robust quantum violations for general $m$ measurements as the number of parties $N$ becomes large. To improve robustness against noise, we optimize the ratio of the quantum value to the classical bound for these families in the large-$N$ limit. We then certify the resulting quantum violation using semidefinite programming. For the broad class of Bell inequalities studied here, the infinite-$N$ ratios take simple rational values for finite $m$ and converge to $\coth(1)$ as $m\to\infty$. The optimized inequalities efficiently detect many-body Bell nonlocality with collective measurements, with more measurement settings leading to stronger violations.

</details>


### [109] [Magic Gate Teleportation: Structure, Useful Resource States, and Simpler Feedforward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08508)
*Yunzhe Zheng,Allen Zang,Aleksander Kubica*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了"魔法门量子隐形传态"(MGT)的理论框架，揭示了其隐藏结构——MGT协议可视为将输入态编码到稳定子码中再执行逻辑非Clifford门。建立了任意交换Pauli旋转资源态的MGT协议构造方法及高效综合算法，证明了有用的MGT资源态必须Clifford等价于对角态，并给出了前馈操作简化为Pauli算子的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子门隐形传态是容错量子计算中的关键技术，但现有研究缺乏对实现非Clifford门（即"魔法"门）的隐形传态协议的系统性理论理解，尤其是在不泄露输入态信息的前提下。本文旨在建立MGT协议的完整理论框架，揭示其内在结构，并为资源态的选择和前馈操作的简化提供理论指导。

Method: 本文采用理论分析方法，利用稳定子形式体系和Pauli回推技术，将MGT协议重新解释为"编码+逻辑非Clifford门"的两步结构。通过分析测量结果与稳定子码编码之间的对应关系，构建了任意交换Pauli旋转态的MGT协议构造方法，并给出了高效的电路综合算法。同时，利用Clifford等价性刻画了有用MGT资源态的充分必要条件。

Result: (1) 揭示了MGT协议的隐藏结构：Pauli测量回推后，MGT可视为将输入态编码到由测量结果标识的稳定子码中，再执行逻辑非Clifford门；(2) 给出了任意交换Pauli旋转态（即对稳定子态施加交换的Pauli旋转所得态）的MGT协议构造方法及高效综合算法；(3) 证明了有用MGT资源态必须Clifford等价于对角态，[[5,1,3]]码蒸馏态对MGT无用；(4) 给出了前馈算子可简化为Pauli算子的条件。

Conclusion: 本文建立了MGT协议的统一理论框架，深化了对非Clifford门隐形传态的理解，为容错量子计算中的资源态设计和前馈操作优化提供了理论依据。特别地，对有用资源态的分类结果对实际量子计算架构中的资源制备策略具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Quantum gate teleportation is a key technique in fault-tolerant quantum computation that uses resource states to implement logical gates. Here, we develop a theory of quantum gate teleportation protocols that implement non-Clifford gates on arbitrary input states without revealing any information about them; we refer to these protocols as magic gate teleportation (MGT). We uncover a hidden structure within MGT -- after backpropagating the Pauli measurements, MGT protocols can be viewed as encoding the input state into a stabilizer code heralded by the measurement outcomes, followed by a logical non-Clifford gate. Using this structure, we construct MGT protocols for any resource state obtained by applying commuting Pauli rotations to a stabilizer state, and provide an efficient algorithm for synthesizing their circuit implementations. Conversely, we prove that useful resource states for MGT, i.e., states that can be used for non-Clifford gates through MGT protocols, are necessarily Clifford-equivalent to diagonal states; in particular, the output state distilled from the $[\![5, 1, 3]\!]$ protocol is not useful for MGT. Finally, we identify conditions under which the feedforward operators can be implemented by Pauli operators, shedding light on the paradigm of algorithmic fault tolerance and simplifying the feedforward operations needed for quantum computing.

</details>


### [110] [Metropolitan entanglement distribution between an atom and a near-visible photon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08513)
*Maya Büki,Pooja Malik,Florian Fertig,Tobias Frank,Marvin Scholz,Tommy Block,Gianvito Chiarella,Yiru Zhou,Emanuele Distante,Pau Farrera,Gerhard Rempe,Harald Weinfurter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过两个定制的低噪声量子频率转换器，将单个原子与共振光子之间的纠缠通过24公里商用光纤（14公里视线距离）成功分发，实现了1.7%的光子传输效率，且对原子-光子纠缠保真度的影响低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络的核心目标是纠缠分发，但先进量子节点的工作波长与电信波段不匹配，而长距离通信依赖光纤基础设施需要电信波段光子，因此需要克服这一波长不匹配问题。

Method: 使用两个定制的低噪声量子频率转换器，将780nm的原子纠缠光子转换为电信S波段，经24km商用光纤传播后再反向转换回原始波长。

Result: 实现了1.7%的光子传输效率，原子-光子纠缠保真度下降小于1%，成功在14km视线距离、24km商用光纤上分发纠缠。

Conclusion: 该方法使得原子量子节点与现有长距离光纤网络的集成成为可能，为量子信息处理中的新型应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Entanglement distribution is the overarching purpose of quantum networks. While communication over long distances can use deployed fiber infrastructure, it requires photons in the telecom band. However, advanced quantum network nodes do not operate at such wavelengths. Here we overcome this limitation with two tailor-made low-noise quantum-frequency converters to distribute entanglement between a single atom and a resonant photon over 14km line-of-sight via 24km of a deployed commercial fiber. The photon at wavelength 780nm is first entangled with the atom, then converted to the telecom S-band, and finally back-converted after propagation through the fiber. This link enables a photon transfer efficiency of 1.7% while affecting the atom-photon entanglement fidelity by less than 1%. This brings integration of atomic quantum nodes with existing long-distance fiber networks into reach, enabling novel applications in quantum information processing.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum Communication Lower Bounds for Search Problems via Matrix Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08517)
*Minbo Gao,Chenghua Liu,Guangxu Yang,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于矩阵不一致性（matrix discrepancy）的新方法，用于证明搜索问题的一向量子通信下界，并首次在碰撞查找和三角形查找问题上建立了紧的量子通信下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索问题不同于判定问题，存在多个有效输出，这使得标准的量子下界技术（如量子 adversary 方法）难以直接应用。现有的量子下界技术对搜索问题的处理能力有限，亟需一种新方法来克服这一根本性障碍。

Method: 提出基于矩阵不一致性（matrix discrepancy）的新方法，通过联合界定量子协议中输出测量的行为，从而绕过搜索问题多输出带来的困难。

Result: 1. 碰撞查找：证明紧的 Ω(N^{1/4}) 一向量子通信下界（此前最好为 Ω(N^{1/12})）。2. 三角形查找：在特定参数条件下（1 ≤ Δ_V ≤ m^{2/3}）得到 Ω(√Δ_V) 的单遍量子流空间下界，匹配经典上界（对数因子内）。3. 该方法还可通过完全不同的论证恢复 Kallaugher and Price 的经典下界，避免了其依赖的 Boolean-Hidden-Matching 归约（该归约对量子协议失效）。

Conclusion: 所提出的矩阵不一致性方法为搜索问题的量子下界提供了通用且有效的工具，填补了量子通信复杂度中搜索问题下界的技术空白，并在多个经典问题上取得了最优或紧的结果。

Abstract: We study one-way quantum communication lower bounds for search problems. Unlike decision problems, search problems can have many valid outputs, which pose a fundamental barrier to standard quantum lower-bound techniques. We overcome this by developing a novel method based on matrix discrepancy, which allows us to bound the output measurements of a quantum protocol jointly.
  As applications of our method, we establish the first tight quantum lower bounds for two fundamental search problems in some natural parameter regimes: collision finding and triangle finding. For collision finding, we prove a tight $Ω(N^{1/4})$ one-way quantum communication lower bound. Previously, the best-known quantum communication lower bound for collision finding was $Ω(N^{1/12})$ due to Göös and Jain (RANDOM 2022), and no stronger bound was known even under the one-way restriction. For triangle finding in graph streams, we prove a one-pass quantum streaming space lower bound of $Ω\left(\sqrt{Δ_V}\right)$ for graphs with $m$ edges, $Θ(m)$ triangles, and constant $Δ_E$, where $Δ_V$ and $Δ_E$ denote the maximum number of triangles sharing a common vertex and edge, respectively, under the condition that $1\le Δ_V\le m^{2/3}$. This constitutes the first nontrivial quantum space lower bound in this regime, matching the classical upper bound of Jayaram and Kallaugher (RANDOM 2021) up to logarithmic factors. Notably, our method also recovers the classical lower bound of Kallaugher and Price (SODA 2017) through an entirely different argument, avoiding their Boolean-Hidden-Matching reduction that breaks down for quantum protocols.

</details>


### [112] [The Langevin-equation description of optomechanics with the dispersive and dissipative optomechanical coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08530)
*Alexander K. Tagantsev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于输入-输出关系方法（经典波动方程）分析光力学系统，并与标准朗之万方程形式主义进行对比。研究发现朗之万方程形式主义在耗散光力耦合情况下存在严重问题：法布里-珀罗腔的衰减率虽与腔长相关，但并未产生耗散光力耦合；输入镜面处的场关系可能不正确；输入场缺少一个对耗散光力耦合至关重要的相位因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的光力学系统通常基于朗之万方程形式主义（唯象引入或基于模型哈密顿量），但在处理半透明机械膜嵌入的法布里-珀罗腔或改进型迈克尔孙-萨格纳克干涉仪时，存在一种无模型的替代方法（基于经典波动方程的输入-输出关系方法）。本文旨在使用后者对朗之万方程形式主义的有效性进行批判性评估，并修正其适用范围。

Method: 采用基于经典波动方程的输入-输出关系方法，推导腔内梯级算符、随机反作用力以及输入镜面处场的关系式；然后将结果简化到朗之万方程形式主义的适用范围内，并进行对比分析。

Result: 1) 法布里-珀罗腔的衰减率虽依赖于腔长，但并未如标准朗之万方程所预测的那样产生耗散光力耦合；2) 在朗之万方程形式主义的适用范围内，输入镜面处的场关系可能不正确；3) 朗之万方程形式主义遗漏了输入场的一个相位因子，该因子在耗散光力耦合情况下至关重要。

Conclusion: 朗之万方程形式主义在处理耗散光力耦合时存在严重问题，其适用范围需要重新审视和修正；输入-输出关系方法能够揭示这些被标准形式主义忽略的物理效应。

Abstract: The description of the optomechanical system is commonly based on the quantum Langevin equation formalism. This framework is introduced phenomenologically or based on a model Hamiltonian. However, once dealing with the optomechanical Fabry-Perot cavity or the modified Michelson-Sagnac interferometer with a semitransparent mechanically active membrane inside, a model-free consideration is also possible by using an alternative approach. Such an approach, which is based on the classical wave equations in the systems, is popular in the gravitational-wave community where it is termed as input-output relations approach. In this work, using the aforementioned approach, we derived the equations for the ladder operator of the intracavity field, stochastic back-action force, and the relation between the fields at the input mirror. Then we simplified the obtained results down to the range of applicability of the Langevin equation formalism and compared these with the corresponding predictions of the latter formalism. This enabled us to critically assess the validity of the Langevin equation formalism and rectify its range of applicability. In the case where the dissipative optomechanical coupling is involved we identified appreciable problems with this formalism. We found that, disregarding the fact that decay rate of the optomechanical Fabry-Perot cavity depends on its length, no dissipative optomechanical coupling is generated. This is in contrast with the prediction of the standard Langevin-equation based treatment. We found that, staying inside the range of applicability of the Langevin equation formalism, the relation between the fields at the input mirror may not be correct. We found that the Langevin equation formalism misses a phase factor at the input field, this factor turns out to be important for the situation involving the dissipative optomechanical coupling.

</details>


### [113] [An Effective Quantum Hoare Logic for Hybrid Quantum Programs with Unbounded Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08548)
*Christophe Chareton,Jad Issa,Romain Péchoux*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合量子程序的半自动化静态分析方法，通过扩展路径和（IHPS）处理无界循环，结合功能验证与资源（终止/成本）估计，并实现了Haskell原型工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合量子程序分析存在空白：符号执行等精确分析技术缺乏对混合化和无界递归的支持，而量子Hoare逻辑虽支持但表达能力不足且缺乏高效等式推理工具。亟需一种既能进行功能验证又能进行资源估算的半自动化解决方案。

Method: 1) 提出整数混合路径和（IHPS），将路径和方法扩展至处理无界while循环，作为程序可能执行的表示；2) 提出基于循环不变量的通用策略，用于确定终止性和期望资源消耗；3) 实现为半自动化的Haskell程序。

Result: 通过多个示例验证了所提策略的有效性，展示了该方法在混合量子程序终止性判定和资源消耗估计上的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作是迈向完整混合量子程序静态资源分析工具的第一步，对实际量子计算应用开发具有重要意义。

Abstract: While quantum hardware remains limited, hybrid quantum-classical algorithms with complex control structures, including unbounded loops, are emerging, posing new challenges for quantum program analysis, including the accurate estimation of the resource consumption of a given program. Meanwhile, precise analysis techniques such as symbolic execution have largely left out hybridization and unbounded recursion. On the other hand, current quantum Hoare logics that generally support them are lacking in expressiveness and miss out on efficient computational equational reasoning that could be implemented in a semi-automated tool. This leaves a gap awaiting to be filled. In this work, we answer this challenge with the first semi-automated static analysis solution combining effective functional verification and resource (termination or cost) estimation for hybrid quantum programs with unbounded loops. Towards that end, we introduce integer hybrid path-sums (IHPS), extending path-sums to handle unbounded while loops, as a representation of possible executions of a program. A generic strategy for determining termination and expected resource consumption via loop invariants is also proposed and illustrated on several examples. Finally, the solution is implemented as a semi-automatic Haskell program. This work is the first step toward the design of a complete static resource analysis tool for hybrid quantum programs, essential for the development of real-world quantum computing.

</details>


### [114] [QSCOUT's Qubit-Boson Gate Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08560)
*Edward C. Tortorici,Ethan C. McGarrigle,Brian K. McFarland,Wes L. Johnson,Daniel S. Lobser,Melissa C. Revelle,Brandon P. Ruzic,Susan M. Clark,Christopher G. Yale*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QSCOUT开发了用于混合连续-离散变量量子计算的量子比特-玻色子门集，并介绍如何使用Jaqal语言在QSCOUT上应用这些门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展量子计算能力，通过混合CV-DV量子计算实现更丰富的量子信息处理。

Method: 在QSCOUT平台上实现量子比特-玻色子门集，并使用Jaqal语言进行编程控制。

Result: 提供了在QSCOUT上利用混合CV-DV门集进行量子计算的具体实现方案。

Conclusion: QSCOUT的量子比特-玻色子门集通过Jaqal语言可被有效地用于混合CV-DV量子计算。

Abstract: The Quantum Scientific Computing Open User Testbed (QSCOUT) has developed a qubit-boson gate set for hybrid continuous-discrete variable (CV-DV) quantum computing. This document outlines how to utilize these gates on QSCOUT using Just Another Quantum Assembly Language, Jaqal\textsuperscript{TM}.

</details>


### [115] [Distributed Monogamy of Entanglement limits Quantum Channel Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08591)
*Rabsan Galib Ahmed,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入分数可扩展性(fractional extendibility)概念，精细刻画量子关联向环境的泄露，证明其张量积不变性和局部处理单调性，并建立分布式纠缠单配性，最终证明擦除概率大于1/2的量子擦除信道无法模拟噪声更低的擦除信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有k-可扩展性仅以整数刻画环境模拟的副本数，缺乏对量子关联泄露程度的精细描述。需要一种更细粒度的量化工具来研究多体纠缠的单配性及其在信道模拟中的应用。

Method: 引入分数可扩展性概念，证明其在张量积下不变、在局部处理下单调；建立分布式纠缠单配性：对AB₁B₂…Bₙ态，从Bᵢ中随机选取k≤n/2个系统，提取EPR对的平均概率上界为k/n。

Result: 利用上述工具证明：任何擦除概率超过1/2的量子擦除信道，即使渐近多次使用，也无法模拟擦除概率更低的信道。

Conclusion: 分数可扩展性提供了比整数可扩展性更精细的量子关联刻画；分布式纠缠单配性建立了多体纠缠共享的严格界限；这些工具在量子信道模拟问题中给出了信道噪声参数间的严格不可模拟性界限。

Abstract: Entanglement is monogamous: if it is shared among more than two parties, the entanglement between any pair cannot be very strong. For an integer $k\geq 2$, $k$-extendibility of a state $ρ_{AB}$ quantifies this as the number of copies of $B$ that can be simulated by the state's environment. We introduce fractional extendibility, which gives a finer characterization of the quantum correlation that is leaked to the environment, and prove that it is invariant under tensor products and monotonic under local processing. We also establish the distributed monogamy of entanglement: for any state on $AB_1B_2\dots B_n$, the maximum average probability of extracting an EPR pair from a random subset of $k \leq n/2$ systems among the $B_i$'s is the fraction $k/n$. With these tools we show that any quantum erasure channel with erasure probability more than a half cannot simulate a less noisy erasure channel, even with asymptotically many uses of the more noisy channel.

</details>


### [116] [A Nonstabilizerness Resource Law for Universal Quantum State Purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08626)
*Keming He,Enji Xiong,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子态纯化所需的魔法资源（magic resources），证明了在奇数维系统中存在精确的线性魔法消耗定律，在多量子比特系统中存在双边线性鲁棒性定律，并展示了魔法资源与保真度之间的定量权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近的no-go结果表明，经典可模拟操作无法实现非平凡的通用保真度增益，这促使研究纯化任务中所需魔法资源的定量理论。

Method: 通过理论分析，对两个输入副本的通用纯化，在奇数维系统中证明了精确的线性魔法定律，在多量子比特系统中证明了双边线性鲁棒性定律（单量子比特情形精确成立），并构造了显式的成功纯化映射。

Result: 建立了通用纯化作为遵循定量魔法-保真度定律的任务，揭示了魔法资源与错误缓解及容错量子信息处理之间的联系。

Conclusion: 本文建立了量子态纯化中魔法资源与保真度之间的定量权衡关系，为理解纯化任务的资源需求提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Quantum state purification aims to recover higher-fidelity quantum states from multiple noisy copies and is a fundamental primitive for quantum information processing. Magic resources enable operations beyond classically simulable dynamics and are central to universal fault-tolerant quantum computation. Recent no-go results show that classically simulable operations cannot achieve a nontrivial universal fidelity gain. This motivates a quantitative theory of the magic required for purification at prescribed success probability and target fidelity. For universal purification with two input copies, we prove an exact linear mana law in odd dimensions and a two-sided linear robustness law for multi-qubit systems, which becomes exact for a single qubit. We also identify an explicit successful purification map that makes the tradeoff transparent. These results establish universal purification as a task obeying a quantitative magic-fidelity law and link magic resources to error mitigation and fault-tolerant quantum information processing.

</details>


### [117] [GroverFigureOfMerit: An Agnostic Figure of Merit for Quantum Backend Characterization in the NISQ Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08636)
*Tiago Restucha,Marcos Guillermo Lammers,Alejandro Fernández*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对NISQ时代量子硬件后端评估缺乏统一指标的问题，提出基于Grover算法的Figure of Merit (FoM)度量，结合成功率、均匀性惩罚和泄漏惩罚，通过Qonscious框架在IBM、IonQ等后端上实现跨平台统一评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NISQ时代硬件提供商通过异构接口暴露能力，静态表征指标（相干时间T1/T2、门错误率）存在三大局限：无法捕捉连续执行间的动态变异性、忽略转译影响、缺乏跨物理架构的可比性，阻碍了开发者对量子后端的知情选择。

Method: 提出GroverFigureOfMerit度量：以Grover算法作为算法级压力测试，组合目标态成功概率、非均匀放大惩罚（参考GRADE方法）和泄漏到非标记态的惩罚，形成统一得分。通过Qonscious框架（条件执行平台+多态适配器）在IBM、IonQ后端及模拟器上无差别执行。

Result: 在理想模拟器和真实处理器噪声模型上的实验证实，该度量能区分不同后端性能并给出统一得分，捕捉了噪声、拓扑结构和转译开销对算法性能的影响。在物理QPU上的验证被识别为下一步自然方向。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了基于Grover算法的跨平台量子后端性能统一评估度量，有效克服了静态指标的局限，系统性分析了九个量子提供商的异构性，推动了通用化量子基准测试的发展。

Abstract: The Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) era poses a challenge for developers: hardware providers expose capabilities through heterogeneous interfaces with proprietary metrics varying widely across providers, hindering informed backend selection. Static characterization metrics - coherence times T1/T2, gate error rates - exhibit limitations: they fail to capture dynamic variability across successive executions, overlook the impact of transpilation, and lack architectural comparability across physically distinct technologies. We propose a Figure of Merit (FoM) based on Grover's algorithm as an algorithmic stress test evaluating quantum backend performance holistically. The metric combines success probability on target states with penalties for non-uniform amplification and leakage to non-marked states, yielding a unified score across hardware architectures. Implemented via the Qonscious framework - a conditional execution platform using polymorphic adapters, it executes agnostically on IBM, IonQ backends, and simulators. Main contributions: (1) proposal and validation of GroverFigureOfMerit, incorporating uniformity and leakage penalties (adapted from GRADE) with emphasis on noise, transpilation, and topological constraints; (2) systematic analysis of heterogeneity across nine quantum providers motivating agnostic metrics; and (3) experimental demonstration via ideal simulators and real-processor noise models, confirming sensitivity to noise, topology, and transpilation overhead. Results confirm the metric distinguishes backend performance under a unified score, capturing intrinsic algorithmic limits. Validation on physical QPUs is identified as a natural next step.

</details>


### [118] [Instability of the undecidable behavior of the spectral gap in 1D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08686)
*Laura Castilla-Castellano,Angelo Lucia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一维谱隙不可判定性构造对扰动极其敏感：任意ε>0下，存在O(ε)范数的1-局域、秩1扰动，可使原不可判定的谱隙问题变为可判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究谱隙不可判定性构造在1维情况下的稳定性，验证其对扰动的敏感程度。

Method: 通过构造1-局域、秩1的微扰（范数O(ε)），分析该扰动如何改变Bausch等人构造的哈密顿量族的谱隙可判定性。

Result: 对于任意ε>0，存在范数为O(ε)的1-局域、秩1扰动，使得原不可判定的谱隙问题变为可判定。

Conclusion: 一维谱隙不可判定性构造对微扰极度敏感，微小扰动即可从根本上改变其计算复杂性性质。

Abstract: The problem of determining the existence of a spectral gap in a lattice quantum spin system was previously shown to be undecidable for one [J. Bausch et al., "Undecidability of the spectral gap in one dimension", Physical Review X 10 (2020)] or more dimensions [T. S. Cubitt et al., "Undecidability of the spectral gap", Nature 528 (2015) and Forum of Mathematics Pi, 10 (2022)]. In this work, we focus on the 1-dimensional result, showing that the constructed family with undecidable behavior is extremely sensitive to perturbations. In particular, for any $\varepsilon > 0$, there exists a 1-local, rank 1, perturbation with norm $O(\varepsilon)$, such that the spectral gap problem for the family in [J. Bausch et al., "Undecidability of the spectral gap in one dimension", Physical Review X 10 (2020)] now becomes decidable.

</details>


### [119] [Low-latency FPGA-based electronic control system for fast preparation of defect-free atom arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08687)
*Ya-Dong Hu,Dong-Qi Ma,Tian-Yang Zhang,Liang Chen,Yi-Chen Zhang,Xiao-Kang Zhong,Wen-Yi Zhu,Hong-Jie Fan,Qing-Xuan Jie,Yan-Lei Zhang,Gang Li,Xi-Feng Ren,Xu-Liang Zhang,Guang-Can Guo,Zhu-Bo Wang,Chang-Ling Zou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于FPGA的电子控制系统，消除了PC在反馈回路中的参与，实现了光子计数、实时决策和波形生成在统一PXIe架构中的集成。系统反馈延迟为282 μs，在24个随机装载的光镊中实现了缺陷-free原子阵列组装，单轮填充率约96%，五轮迭代后10原子阵列成功概率从65.7%提升至95.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子量子计算的可扩展性要求集成化的电子控制系统，具备低延迟、模块化架构和实时反馈能力，以支持量子纠错和中间电路测量。

Method: 采用FPGA作为核心处理器，基于PXIe统一架构集成光子计数、实时决策与波形生成功能，消除了传统PC在反馈回路中的延迟瓶颈。

Result: 系统总反馈延迟为282 μs；单轮重排填充率约96%；五轮反馈控制迭代后，生成10原子缺陷-free阵列的成功概率从65.7%提升至95.4%。

Conclusion: 该系统建立了中性原子平台实现中间电路测量和实时量子纠错所需的电子基础设施。

Abstract: The scalability of neutral atom quantum computing demands integrated electronic control systems with low latency, modular architecture, and real-time feedback capability. Here, we present an FPGA-based electronic control system that eliminates the PC from the feedback loop, integrating photon counting, real-time decision-making, and waveform generation within a unified PXIe architecture. The system achieves a total feedback latency of $282\,\mathrm{μs}$ and is validated in practical experiments by assembling defect-free atom arrays from 24 stochastically loaded optical tweezers. A single-round rearrangement achieves a filling fraction of $\sim96\%$, while feedback-controlled iterative rearrangement over five rounds boosts the success probability for generating a 10-atom defect-free array from $65.7\%$ to $95.4\%$. This system establishes the electronic infrastructure necessary for mid-circuit measurement and real-time quantum error correction on neutral-atom platforms.

</details>


### [120] [Quantifying randomness with measurement incompatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08697)
*Sebastian Schlösser,Pauli Jokinen,Martin Plávala,Leevi Leppäjärvi,Leonardo S. V. Santos,Roope Uola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在制备-测量场景中，观测到的测量不相容性与经典窃听者能力之间的权衡关系，建立了不相容性与随机性生成的定性联系，并利用不相容性见证作为随机性证书，通过半定规划程序用广义鲁棒性这一几何度量来约束Eve的策略，同时提供了从任意不相容测量集合生成随机性的显式协议。该结果可推广至量子导引和Eve拥有量子存储的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索测量不相容性与随机性生成之间的定量关系，并利用这种关系来约束窃听者（Eve）在制备-测量场景中的能力。

Method: 建立了不相容性与随机性生成之间的定性联系，使用不相容性见证作为随机性证书，将不相容性的几何度量——广义鲁棒性——通过半定规划来约束Eve的策略，并提供了从任何不相容测量集合生成随机性的显式协议。进一步将结果推广到量子导引和Eve拥有量子存储的场景。

Result: 测量不相容性越大，可生成的随机性越多，从而对Eve策略的约束越强；在量子导引场景中，导引能力与随机性生成之间存在紧密联系；该技术可推广到Eve拥有量子存储的场景。

Conclusion: 测量不相容性与随机性生成之间存在定量权衡关系，这种关系可用于安全性证明和量子信息协议设计。

Abstract: We present a trade-off between the amount of observed measurement incompatibility and the capabilities of a classical Eavesdropper in a prepare-and-measure scenario. The result is based on a qualitative connection between measurement incompatibility and randomness generation together with the utilization of incompatibility witnesses as randomness certificates. This allows one to use a geometric measure of incompatibility, the generalised robustness, to bound Eve's strategies through a semi-definite program, while providing an explicit protocol for generating randomness from any set of incompatible measurements. By translating the result to quantum steering, we find a tight connection between steerability and randomness generation in a setting using any finite number of measurement inputs. We further show how our techniques can be generalised to scenarios where Eve has a quantum memory by using a dimensional generalisation of joint measurability.

</details>


### [121] [Robust One-Sided Device-Independent Quantum Key Distribution via High-Dimensional Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08709)
*Monika Mothsara,Suraj Goel,Bohnishikha Ghosh,Vatshal Srivastav,Will McCutcheon,Mehul Malik,Gláucia Murta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的高维单边设备无关量子密钥分发（HD 1sDI-QKD）协议，利用横向空间自由度纠缠光子进行实验验证，通过量子导引进行安全分析，展示了增加维度可增强抗噪性和抗损耗能力，并在维度d=7时获得最高密钥速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子密钥分发虽具有信息论安全性，但在实践中易受噪声、损耗和器件不完美影响，现有方案难以同时满足高安全性和实用性要求。

Method: 采用光子横向空间自由度纠缠的高维1sDI-QKD协议，利用量子导引进行系统安全分析，评估不同测量配置和系统维度下的密钥速率，使用反向协调方案。

Result: 提高维度可增强对噪声和损耗的鲁棒性；在公平采样假设下所有实验维度均获得正密钥速率，维度d=7时密钥速率最高。

Conclusion: 该工作为高维1sDI-QKD提供了完整的安全分析框架和实验验证，并讨论了实现无漏洞实用化QKD的后续步骤。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) brings the promise of communication with information-theoretic security but is limited in practice due to its susceptibility to noise, losses, and device imperfections. To address these challenges, we propose a robust high-dimensional (HD) one-sided device-independent QKD (1sDI-QKD) protocol and present a proof-of-principle experimental implementation using photons entangled in the transverse-spatial degree-of-freedom. We develop a systematic security analysis of HD 1sDI-QKD protocols, leveraging quantum steering to certify security, and evaluate achievable secret key rates for different measurement configurations and system dimensions using reverse reconciliation. Our analysis shows that increasing the dimension enhances robustness against both noise and loss. We then demonstrate the key experimental building blocks required for implementing the protocol: (a) a high-quality source of high-dimensional photonic entanglement, and (b) a fully programmable, high-dimensional multi-outcome measurement device operating in up to dimension 11. Using these components, we obtain positive key rates for all investigated dimensions under the fair-sampling assumption, with the highest key rates achieved for dimension d=7. Finally, we discuss the steps required for a practical, loophole-free implementation of 1sDI-QKD in realistic regimes of loss and noise.

</details>


### [122] [Typicality of Steering for Two-qubit States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08762)
*Gerard Anglès Munné,Paweł Cieśliński,Tamás Vértesi,Wiesław Laskowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了两量子比特系统中量子导引的典型性行为。通过解析推导和数值分析，发现随机量子态在有限测量设置下表现出高概率的导引性，且随着测量设置数增加，导引概率趋近于100%，显著高于贝尔非定域性的相应概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子导引是一种独特的量子效应，对量子通信和计算具有重要意义。理解量子导引在一般量子态中的典型行为——即随机两比特态在随机投影测量下表现出导引性的概率——对于评估其作为量子资源的普适性和实用性至关重要。

Method: 推导了Werner态在二设置和三设置场景（后者限于Bloch球共面投影测量）下导引概率的解析表达式；对更多测量设置和不同随机态系综进行了数值分析，并与贝尔非定域性的概率进行了比较。

Result: 导引概率随测量设置数系统性增加；在有限测量设置下，随机态表现出高导引概率；随着设置数增加，导引概率趋近100%，显著高于贝尔非定域性的对应概率；不同态系综（如Bell对角态、Werner态）中识别出了具有最大非经典潜力的态。

Conclusion: 与环境耦合最小的随机态在有限测量设置下具有高导引概率，并随着测量设置数增加趋向典型性（100%），表明量子导引作为资源在量子协议中具有广泛适用性和可靠性。

Abstract: Phenomena that slip beyond the grasp of our classical intuition reveal uniquely quantum effects that deepen our understanding of the physical world and enable advances in information processing, particularly in quantum communication and computation. One such phenomenon is quantum steering, whereby measurements performed by one party influence the conditional states of another when the two share an entangled quantum system. If the observed correlations cannot be explained by a local hidden state model, the state is said to be steerable. In this work, we investigate the typicality of this behavior: given a generic two-qubit state and $m$ Haar-random projective measurements, what is the probability of observing steering? We derive analytical expressions for the steering probability $\mathcal{P}_S$ of Werner states in two- and three-setting scenarios, the latter restricted to coplanar projective measurements on the Bloch sphere. For larger numbers of settings and various random states ensembles, we perform numerical analyses showing that $\mathcal{P}_S$ increases systematically with the number of measurements and substantially exceeds the corresponding probabilities associated with Bell nonlocality. Our results demonstrate that random states with minimal environmental coupling exhibit a high probability of steering for finite $m$ and approach genuine typicality, $\mathcal{P}_S=100\%$, as the number of settings increases. We provide a detailed characterization of $\mathcal{P}_S$ across different state ensembles and specific families, including Bell-diagonal and Werner states, identifying those with the greatest non-classical potential and highlighting their relevance for protocols in which steering serves as a key resource.

</details>


### [123] [Plaquette: A hardware-aware design platform for fault-tolerant quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08767)
*Raul Conchello Vendrell,Carlos Díaz López,Ish Dhand,Kshitij Kapoor,Davide Laureti,Marcello Massaro,Pranjal Nayak,Ivan Ogloblin,Martin B. Plenio,Shreya Prasanna Kumar,Matteo Santandrea,Varun Seshadri,Antal Száva,Trevor Vincent,Raphael Weber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Plaquette是一个理论框架和软件套件，能够将硬件噪声模型（如超导量子比特泄漏、中性原子散射、离子阱加热等非Pauli噪声）自动编译为不同保真度的模拟器所需格式，从而直接计算容错量子架构的逻辑性能。实验验证表明，在非Pauli噪声下，XPauli采样器和近Clifford采样器能匹配全态模拟结果，而单纯Clifford模拟可能产生显著偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件团队构建容错量子计算机时必须决定抑制哪些不完美因素，这需要了解架构在真实器件噪声下的逻辑性能。然而硬件噪声往往偏离可扩展稳定子模拟器使用的随机Pauli模型，现有工具无法直接从物理噪声计算容错架构的逻辑性能。

Method: 提出Plaquette框架，将硬件误差模型（Kraus算子、Hamiltonian-Lindblad动力学或实验重构的量子信道）自动编译为四类采样器所需格式：稳定子采样（Pauli噪声）、XPauli采样器（泄漏与环境）、近Clifford采样器（相干误差）和全态模拟（精确参考）。

Result: XPauli和近Clifford采样器在全态模拟的统计不确定度内匹配结果，而Pauli twirling在某些误差模型下会偏离。在超导量子比特泄漏、中性原子散射和离子阱加热三种误差模型上验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: Plaquette提供了从器件开放系统物理到FTQC逻辑性能的直接路径。平台和噪声过程不同会导致Plaquette与仅Clifford模拟之间的差异大小不同，因此可靠的阈值、错误预算和开销估计需要最精确的可用模拟。

Abstract: Hardware teams building fault-tolerant quantum computers (FTQCs) must decide which imperfections to suppress, and that decision requires the logical performance of the architecture under the device's actual noise. Hardware noise often departs from the stochastic Pauli models used by scalable stabilizer simulators: superconducting transmons leak out of the computational subspace, neutral atoms scatter through intermediate states, trapped ions heat as their motional modes absorb phonons, and miscalibrated controls over-rotate coherently. We present Plaquette, a theoretical framework and software suite that computes the logical performance of fault-tolerant architectures directly from the physics of such imperfections. In Plaquette, a hardware error model is specified once, as Kraus operators, Hamiltonian-Lindblad dynamics, or an experimentally reconstructed quantum channel, and is compiled automatically into the exact or approximate representation required by each of four sampler classes: stabilizer sampling for Pauli noise, the new XPauli sampler for leakage and environment sectors, near-Clifford samplers for coherent errors, and full-state simulation for exact reference calculations. We validate the XPauli and near-Clifford samplers against full-state simulation, which they can match within statistical uncertainty while Pauli twirling can fall short depending on the error model. We demonstrate the framework on three error models: leakage in superconducting qubits, intermediate-state scattering in neutral atoms, and heating in trapped ions. The size of the discrepancy between Plaquette and Clifford-only simulations varies with platform and noise process, so reliable thresholds, error budgets, and overhead estimates require the most accurate simulation available. Plaquette provides a direct path from the open-system physics of a device to the logical performance of the FTQC built on it.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Gravitational perturbations of a regular T-duality inspired black hole: Quasinormal modes, excitation factors, and time-domain evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07715)
*Bekir Can Lütfüoğlu,Mardon Abdullaev,Javlon Rayimbaev,Shokhzod Jumaniyozov,Shavkat Karshiboev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由T-对偶性启发的非局域零长理论产生的正则黑洞的轴向引力扰动，发现零长参数使得黑洞环鸣振荡频率加快（实部增大），阻尼率先微增后减小，且激发因子变化比频率更平缓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨非局域、T-对偶性启发的零长理论所生成的正则黑洞在引力扰动下的动力学行为，以理解零长参数如何修正标准Schwarzschild黑洞的环鸣信号。

Method: 使用高阶WKB-Padé方法计算基模和多个泛音，并通过时域演化验证主导模；同时计算激发因子和有效势。

Result: 零长参数开启后，ADM标度的ℓ=2,3,4引力模频率实部增大（环鸣振荡更快）；阻尼率先略增后在大变形参数下减小；有效势垒升高；激发因子幅度变化远小于频率变化。

Conclusion: 零长参数对黑洞环鸣频率有显著影响，但激发因子相对稳定，这一行为与有效势垒升高一致。

Abstract: We study axial gravitational perturbations of the neutral regular black hole generated by a non-local, T-duality-inspired zero-point length and the associated gravitational self-energy. In this geometry, the usual point source is replaced by a regular core, and the zero-point length controls the departure from the Schwarzschild limit. We compute the fundamental quasinormal modes and several overtones using high-order WKB--Padé methods, and we verify the dominant mode via direct time-domain evolution. When the zero-point length is turned on, the real parts of the ADM-scaled frequencies increase for the gravitational modes with $\ell=2,3,4$, so the ringdown oscillates faster than in the Schwarzschild limit. The damping rates change more gradually: they initially increase slightly and then decrease near the largest deformation values considered here. This behavior is consistent with the effective potential, whose barrier becomes higher as the deformation parameter increases. We also compute the corresponding excitation factors and find that their magnitudes vary much less strongly than the quasinormal frequencies.

</details>


### [125] [Deformed Compact Objects in General Relativity and Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07736)
*Jonathan Tejeda Quartuccio*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本论文研究了中子星等致密天体在不同引力理论（广义相对论、D-TOV变形方案、f(R,T)引力）下的流体静力学平衡，分析了变形参数和物质-几何耦合对恒星质量、半径等全局性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密天体是研究超核密度物质和强场引力的独特实验室，需要在不同几何和引力场景下深入理解其平衡结构。

Method: 推导了广义相对论中的标准TOV方程，引入有效单参数变形方案得到D-TOV形式，并将流体静力学平衡框架扩展到f(R,T)引力。使用GM1状态方程、MIT袋模型和多幂律状态方程计算了中子星和奇异星的平衡序列。

Result: 在广义相对论中，变形参数D显著影响恒星性质：扁球构型支撑更大质量和半径，长球构型导致质量更小、更致密。在f(R,T)模型中，依赖于迹的物质-几何耦合进一步改变质量-半径关系和最大支撑质量。

Conclusion: 有效变形和依赖于迹的物质-几何耦合能够显著影响致密星的平衡结构。

Abstract: Neutron stars and related compact objects are unique laboratories for probing matter at supranuclear densities and gravity in the strong-field regime. In this thesis, we investigate the hydrostatic equilibrium of compact stars in different geometric and gravitational scenarios, combining relativistic stellar structure, dense-matter microphysics, and numerical modeling. We derive the standard Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equation in General Relativity, introduce an effective one-parameter deformation scheme leading to the deformed TOV (D-TOV) formalism, and extend the hydrostatic equilibrium framework to f(R,T) gravity.
  Using these structure equations, we compute equilibrium sequences for neutron stars and strange stars described by the GM1 equation of state, the MIT Bag Model, and a polytropic equation of state. In General Relativity, the deformation parameter D significantly affects the global stellar properties: oblate configurations generally support larger masses and radii than the spherical case, while prolate configurations lead to less massive and more compact stars. In optimized and linear f(R,T) models, trace-dependent matter-geometry couplings further modify the mass-radius relation and the maximum supported mass. Overall, the results indicate that effective deformation and trace-dependent matter-geometry coupling can significantly affect the equilibrium structure of compact stars.

</details>


### [126] [Systematic Light Propagation Bias from the Heliosphere and Its Impact on the Hubble Tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07741)
*Behnam Pourhassan,İzzet Sakallı,Sudhaker Upadhyay,Kairat Myrzakulov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出哈勃张力（本地测量H₀=73.5 vs CMB预测H₀=67.4）的约3-8%可能源于太阳系日球层环境对本地距离测量的系统效应，而非新物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈勃张力是现代宇宙学中最显著的矛盾之一，现有理论难以完全解释，需探索可能的系统误差来源。

Method: 通过理论计算分析太阳系日球层（HS）产生的加热等离子体和高能中性原子（ENAs）环境对造父变星和Ia型超新星观测的系统影响，考虑各向异性、部分校准抵消和色抑制等因素。

Result: HS效应可解释观测到的8-9%哈勃张力中的最多约8%，实际贡献在3-8%范围内。

Conclusion: 哈勃张力可能部分源于日球层环境对本地距离测量的系统误差，而非全新物理，并提出了验证该假说的观测测试方案。

Abstract: The Hubble tension (HT) represents one of the most significant discrepancies in modern cosmology, with local distance measurements yielding $H_0 = 73.5 \pm 1.4$ km/s/Mpc while cosmic microwave background (CMB) observations predict $H_0 = 67.4 \pm 0.5$ km/s/Mpc. We propose that this tension arises from systematic effects introduced by our Solar System's heliospheric (HS) environment on local distance measurements. The HS creates a complex medium of heated plasma and energetic neutral atoms (ENAs) beyond the heliopause (HP), where interstellar medium (ISM) temperatures rise significantly. This thermal gradient and particle environment may systematically affect observations of Cepheid variables and Type Ia supernovae (SNe~Ia) used in the local cosmic distance ladder (CDL), biasing distance measurements and artificially inflating the measured Hubble constant. We present theoretical calculations showing how HS effects could account for up to $\sim 8\%$ of the observed 8--9\% discrepancy, with the realistic contribution lying in the $\sim 3$--$8\%$ range once anisotropy, partial calibration cancellation, and chromatic suppression are included, and discuss observational tests to validate this hypothesis.

</details>


### [127] [Observational Limits on Einasto Dark Matter Parameters from Event Horizon Telescope Images of Sgr A$^{*}$ and M87$^{*}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07752)
*A. Errehymy,S. Hansraj,C. Hansraj*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用事件视界望远镜（EHT）对Sgr A*和M87*的阴影观测，通过构建包含Einasto型暗物质晕的静态球对称黑洞度规，对暗物质内晕结构参数进行了贝叶斯约束。结果表明，对Sgr A*的暗物质密度上限约为10^{-11} M⊙/pc³（1σ置信度），而M87*的约束较弱；Einasto指数约束力有限，说明EHT精度主要限制光子球附近的质量而非剖面斜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHT对超大质量黑洞的直接成像为检验引力理论和探测黑洞周围物质分布提供了新窗口。暗物质晕等延展质量分布会扰动光子环区域的零测地线，从而影响黑洞阴影。因此，通过阴影测量可以探究暗物质内晕结构，补充天体动力学和引力透镜等传统方法。

Method: 从Einasto密度分布出发，基于Xu等人(2018)的方法构建包含黑洞和暗物质晕的度规函数f(r)=1-2M/r+2M_∞\tilde{g}(r)，分析非自旋极限下的光子势、零测地线和阴影半径。利用EHT测量的无量纲阴影直径d_sh进行贝叶斯参数估计，结合独立测量的黑洞质量先验，确定Einasto参数空间的允许区域。

Result: 对Sgr A*，采用恒星轨道质量先验时，暗物质密度上限为ρ_0≲10^{-11} M⊙/pc³（1σ置信度）；对M87*，由于距离不确定性约束较弱。Einasto指数ν约束力不足，表明EHT精度主要限制光子球附近的致密质量而非密度剖面斜率。

Conclusion: EHT阴影数据可以有效约束黑洞周围暗物质的内晕密度上限，但对剖面形状参数区分度有限。未来更高精度的EHT观测将进一步完善这些约束，并有助于区分不同暗物质模型。

Abstract: The Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) has provided images of the supermassive black-holes Sgr A$^{*}$ and M87$^{*}$, enabling direct tests of gravity. Any extended mass-distribution, such as a dark-matter halo, perturbs null-geodesics in the photon-ring regime, making shadow-measurements a probe of inner-halo structure. In this work, we investigate static, spherically-symmetric black-holes surrounded by Einasto-type dark-matter halos and derive constraints from EHT shadow-data. Starting from the Einasto density-profile with parameters ${\varrho_0, \tildeα, \tildeν}$, we construct a metric-function $f(r)=1-2M/r+2M_\infty \tilde{g}(r)$ that interpolates between the black-hole horizon and the asymptotic halo, following the approach of Xu et al. (2018) but adapted specifically to the Einasto scenario. We analyze the photon-potential, null-geodesics, and shadow-radius as functions of black-hole mass $M$, in the non-spinning limit. Using the dimensionless shadow-diameter $d_{\text{sh}}\equiv Dθ/M$ measured by the EHT -- $d_{\text{sh}}^{M87*}=11.0\pm1.5$ and $d_{\text{sh}}^{SgrA*}=9.5\pm1.4$ -- we perform Bayesian parameter-estimation to identify allowed regions in the Einasto parameter-space. Combined with the independently-measured black-hole masses from stellar-dynamics, our results place constraints on the inner dark-matter distribution: for Sgr A$^{*}$, adopting the stellar-orbit mass-prior, we find $\varrho_0 \lesssim 10^{-11},M_\odot/\text{pc}^3$ at $1σ$ confidence, while for M87$^{*}$ the bounds are weaker due to distance-uncertainties. The Einasto-index $\tildeν$ is weakly-constrained, indicating that EHT precision primarily limits the mass enclosed near the photon-sphere rather than the profile-slope. Future EHT observations will refine these constraints and distinguish between competing dark-matter descriptions.

</details>


### [128] [Regular Black Holes in Nonlocal Quasitopological Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07790)
*Pablo Bueno,Pablo A. Cano,Robie A. Hennigar,Ángel J. Murcia*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了拟拓扑引力的无限微分完备化，在无鬼、无强耦合不稳定性条件下，获得了满足微扰Birkhoff定理的精确球对称真空规则黑洞解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟拓扑引力本身存在高阶导数导致的鬼场和强耦合不稳定性问题，需要寻找一种完备化机制来克服这些理论缺陷。

Method: 采用无限微分（非局域）算子替代有限高阶导数项，构建拟拓扑引力的完备化理论，并求解球对称真空场方程。

Result: 得到了精确的球对称真空规则黑洞解（即无奇点黑洞解），该解满足微扰Birkhoff定理，且理论无鬼场、无强耦合不稳定性。

Conclusion: 无限微分完备化是解决拟拓扑引力中鬼场和强耦合问题的有效途径，并能自然导出规则黑洞解，为量子引力候选理论提供了重要支持。

Abstract: We present infinite-derivative completions of Quasitopological gravities that are ghost-free, avoid strong coupling instabilities and admit exact, spherically symmetric vacuum regular-black-hole solutions satisfying a perturbative Birkhoff theorem.

</details>


### [129] [Singularity resolution and unitarity in two-dimensional dilaton black holes with negative central charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07806)
*César García-Pérez,F. Javier Marañón-González,José Navarro-Salas,Silvia Pla*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究一阶量子修正的CGHS二维膨胀引力模型，通过引入Polyakov型作用量和局域抵消项，在负总中心荷条件下消除了经典曲率奇点，得到了内部渐近平坦区域。外部Hawking通量得以保留并与内部辐射通量存在关联，暗示可能保持幺正性，但能量守恒问题尚未完全解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CGHS模型中存在曲率奇点和信息丢失问题，作者希望通过量子修正（一阶圈修正）来研究奇点是否被消除、信息是否得以保存，从而探索量子引力中黑洞信息悖论的解决方案。

Method: 对经典CGHS模型进行一阶圈修正，有效作用量包含三部分：描述物质涨落的非局域Polyakov作用量、基于辅助平直度量实现Faddeev-Popov重参数化鬼影Strominger机制的Polyakov型项、以及保持辅助度量平直性并确保精确可解性和Minkowski时空解存在的局域抵消项。

Result: 在负总中心荷条件下，经典曲率奇点被消除，视界内部出现渐近平坦区域；外部Hawking通量保持不变，并与从视界内部逼近未来零无穷的内部辐射通量存在关联；内部辐射通量在短时区间内呈现负值。

Conclusion: 内外辐射通量的关联性暗示系统可能保持幺正性（前提是相关零曲面与塌缩物质轨迹保持有限仿射距离），但当前框架下尚未建立完全一致的能量平衡，需要进一步研究解决该问题。

Abstract: We study a one-loop corrected extension of the classical Callan-Giddings-Harvey-Strominger (CGHS) model of two-dimensional dilaton gravity. The effective action combines the non-local Polyakov action for matter fluctuations, a Polyakov-type term built from an auxiliary flat metric that implements Strominger's mechanism for the Faddeev-Popov reparametrization ghosts, and a local counterterm that simultaneously preserves the flatness of the auxiliary metric, ensures exact solvability, and keeps two-dimensional Minkowski spacetime as an exact solution of the backreacted equations. In the regime of negative total central charge, the classical curvature singularity is resolved and gives way to asymptotically flat regions inside the horizon. The exterior Hawking flux is preserved and turns out to be correlated with an internal radiation flux supported on null surfaces that approach null infinity from beyond the horizon; this internal flux, in particular, presents a short interval of negative values. These correlations point to the preservation of unitarity, provided the relevant null surfaces remain at a finite affine distance from the collapsing matter trajectory. Within the present formulation, however, a fully consistent energy balance cannot yet be established. We discuss possible strategies to overcome this issue.

</details>


### [130] [Causal Horizons, Geodesic Completeness and Stability in Slow Contraction Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07815)
*Mariam Khaldieh,Anna I. Rosenzweig,Paul J. Steinhardt*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 慢收缩（ekpyrosis）宇宙学模型具有半无限慢收缩相，该相以闵可夫斯基空间为稳定过去吸引子，并以平坦、均匀、各向同性且Weyl曲率可忽略的状态为稳定未来吸引子，从而避免了粒子视界问题、因果视界问题，生成了真空涨落谱，并因沿过去类测地线的平均膨胀率非正而满足Borde-Guth-Vilenkin定理的条件，实现了过去测地线完备性。而收缩德西特空间则存在有限粒子视界且在标量场、物质或辐射下不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准宇宙大爆炸模型及收缩德西特空间存在视界问题、初始奇点问题（BGV定理）以及初始条件精细调节等基础性难题，作者试图探索一种能够同时解决这些问题的宇宙学模型。

Method: 通过理论分析，研究具有半无限慢收缩相（ekpyrosis）的宇宙学模型的动力学吸引子性质、粒子视界、量子涨落生成以及沿过去类测地线的几何完备性，并与收缩德西特空间进行对比。

Result: 慢收缩相存在稳定的过去吸引子（渐近闵可夫斯基空间）和稳定的未来吸引子（平坦、均匀、各向同性、Weyl曲率可忽略）；无粒子视界，从而避开因果视界问题；过去闵可夫斯基吸引子可在所有波长上产生真空涨落谱；平均膨胀率非正，满足BGV定理条件，实现过去测地线完备性。而收缩德西特空间存在有限粒子视界，在标量场、物质或辐射下不稳定。

Conclusion: 慢收缩宇宙学模型兼具多项优良性质，可同时解决视界问题、初始奇点问题和初始条件精细调节问题，是比收缩德西特空间或标准大爆炸膨胀更优越的理论框架。

Abstract: We show that cosmological models with a semi-infinite phase of slow contraction (ekpyrosis) possess a combination of properties that can address several fundamental problems in cosmology, otherwise faced in contracting de Sitter phases or standard big bang expansion. In particular, slow contraction admits a stable past attractor asymptoting to Minkowski space, as well as a stable, flat, homogeneous, and isotropic future attractor with negligible Weyl curvature (and, therefore, negligible gravitational entropy). In bouncing cosmologies, this contracting attractor is terminated by a smooth, non-singular bounce that transforms the attractor properties at the end of contraction into the initial conditions for the subsequent expanding phase. Cosmologies incorporating a slow contraction phase have no particle horizon and therefore avoid the causal horizon problem. The past Minkowski attractor also generates an initial spectrum of vacuum-like quantum fluctuations on all wavelengths. Moreover, because the averaged expansion rate along past-directed geodesics is non-positive, models incorporating a semi-infinite phase of slow contraction also evade the Borde,Guth and Vilenkin theorem and are past geodesically complete. By contrast, contracting de Sitter space possesses a finite particle horizon and becomes unstable in the presence of scalar fields, matter, or radiation.

</details>


### [131] [Charging up regular black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07831)
*Raúl Carballo-Rubio,Chiara Coviello,Vania Vellucci*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种构造带电正则黑洞的一般性方法，证明了Bardeen和Hayward黑洞在带电时会变成奇异，并提出了改进的带电正则版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的正则黑洞模型（如Bardeen和Hayward黑洞）在非带电情况下是正则的，但在带电时可能失去正则性。作者旨在建立一个一般性框架来构造带电正则黑洞，并检验已有模型在带电情况下的正则性。

Method: 通过推广爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦场方程，将爱因斯坦张量变形为包含引力场二阶导数的守恒张量，在球对称性下构造一般性的带电正则黑洞解。

Result: 带电情况下正则性条件比非带电情况更严格。Bardeen和Hayward黑洞在带电时变为奇异。作者提出了改进的Bardeen和Hayward度规，使其在带电时保持正则。

Conclusion: 真空解的规则化不足以推广到其他物理情形（如带电、旋转、存在物质场）。构建真实的物理正则黑洞需要更严格的约束条件。

Abstract: We present a general construction of charged regular black holes as solutions of a generalization of the Einstein--Maxwell field equations in spherical symmetry in which the Einstein tensor is deformed into an identically conserved tensor containing up to second derivatives of the gravitational field. The generality of the construction allows us to define the field equations satisfied by generic regular black holes when becoming charged. The conditions that guarantee regularity of charged solutions are evaluated and shown to be more stringent than the regularity conditions for uncharged solutions. This implies, in particular, that the charged versions of the Bardeen and Hayward black holes become singular. Improved versions of the Bardeen and Hayward metrics that remain regular when charged are proposed. Our results indicate that regularizing the vacuum solutions of general relativity is, in general, not enough to yield regular solutions in other situations of physical interest. The implications that follow for the construction of realistic regular black holes, in which aspects such as rotation and the presence of matter fields are taken into account, are discussed.

</details>


### [132] [Finite mass two throat wormholes: global light rings, branch resolved strong lensing, and scalar transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07839)
*Anirudh Pradhan,K. Ghaderi,M. Zeyauddin,K. Karimizadeh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个静态、无视界、渐近平直的双喉虫洞族，其中单一全局度量决定了几何、有效源、零动力学、透镜效应和测试场传播。通过有限质量形变四阶嵌入轮廓，产生两个对称喉部和中间赤道，Misner-Sharp质量在两端渐近趋于相同有限值。该模型在单一时空中统一描述了拓扑、平均能量条件、分支分辨强透镜和波传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个有限质量的虫洞模型，使得拓扑结构、能量条件、强引力透镜和波传播能在单一度规框架下统一描述，并研究其动力学和观测特征。

Method: 通过有限质量形变四阶嵌入轮廓构造双喉虫洞族，利用单一全局度量描述几何；分析Misner-Sharp质量和爱因斯坦张量；计算径向平均零泛函和全局零势垒进行相分类；解析推导对数强偏折系数并进行数值验证；研究标量散射问题。

Result: 全局不稳定集可分为喉环、四喉外环或亚临界赤道环等相态；四环相中所有不稳定环具有相同冲击尺度但内外分支Lyapunov指数不同；跨喉偏折系数为光线穿越所有全局最大值的贡献之和；标量散射呈现相位相关势垒和共振传输。

Conclusion: 该模型在有限质量框架下统一了拓扑结构、平均能量条件、分支分辨强透镜和波传输，为虫洞物理提供了完整的理论分析工具。

Abstract: We introduce a static, horizonless, asymptotically flat two throat wormhole family in which one global metric determines the geometry, effective source, null dynamics, lensing, and test field propagation. A finite mass deformation of a quartic embedding profile produces two symmetric throats and an intermediate equator, while the associated Misner--Sharp mass tends to the same finite value at both asymptotic ends. The temporal sector yields the same mass parameter, and the Einstein tensor defines a single conserved anisotropic source. In addition to the local flare out identity, the complete radial averaged null functional is obtained exactly and is strictly negative. The global null potential admits a complete phase classification for all positive masses and nonnegative redshift deformation. Depending on the parameters, the global unstable set consists of throat rings, four off throat rings, or exterior rings accompanied by a subcritical equatorial ring. In the four ring phase, all unstable rings have the same impact scale but the inner and outer branches have different Lyapunov exponents. We derive the logarithmic strong deflection coefficients analytically and show that the cross throat coefficient is the sum of the contributions from every global maximum traversed by the ray. Direct numerical integration verifies these coefficients. The scalar scattering problem exhibits phase dependent barriers and resonant transmission. The resulting model provides a finite mass setting in which topology, averaged energy conditions, branch resolved strong lensing, and wave transmission are derived from one spacetime.

</details>


### [133] [Nonlinear hydrodynamics in spinning neutron stars: Theoretical universal relations and equilibrium solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07943)
*Hang Yu,Giorgio Nicolini,Shu Yan Lau,K. J. Kwon,Tejaswi Venumadhav,Nils Andersson,Pantelis Pnigouras,Fabian Gittins,Amlan Nanda*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究双中子星并合过程中的非线性潮汐效应，利用仿射近似将中子星视为椭球体，在哈密顿量中解析推导至四波阶的耦合系数。发现三波相互作用系数完全由线性潮汐决定，不包含新物理，但忽略它会导致引力波形显著误差（单星相位偏移约1.7弧度）。四波相互作用在慢自旋时贡献很小，但在快速自旋时可通过非线性离心驱动研究内部绝热指数这一线性潮汐无法探测的物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双中子星并合过程中的潮汐效应是引力波探测的重要信息来源，但非线性流体动力学相互作用尚未被充分理解。现有模型多局限于线性潮汐，而高阶非线性效应（如三波、四波相互作用）对引力波波形的影响以及能否揭示中子星内部新物理尚不清楚。

Method: 采用仿射近似（affine approximation）将受扰中子星视为椭球体，在哈密顿体系中解析推导f模和径向模至四波阶（next-to-next-to-leading order）的耦合系数，允许背景星体任意自转。采用混合方法同时捕捉牛顿流体动力学中的模式共振，并与低频展开下的相对论计算结果保持一致。

Result: 三波相互作用系数完全由线性潮汐性质决定，不包含新物理。但忽略三波非线性潮汐会导致引力波形显著系统误差，单星非线性潮汐相比线性模型在并合前累积约1.7弧度相位偏移，双星系统相位偏移约翻倍。四波相互作用在慢自旋时修正很小；快速自旋时，f模的非线性离心驱动和四波非谐性可探测与内部浮力相关的绝热指数。四波非谐性不会导致f模的共振锁定。

Conclusion: 非线性潮汐效应对于精确引力波建模至关重要，其中三波相互作用虽不蕴含新物理但不可忽略；四波相互作用在快速自旋中子星中为探测星体内部绝热指数提供了新窗口。

Abstract: We study tides during the inspiral of a binary neutron star (BNS) system, including nonlinear hydrodynamical interactions. Using an affine approximation that treats the perturbed NS as an ellipsoid, we analytically derive coupling coefficients among the f-modes and the radial mode to the four-wave order (i.e., next-to-next-to-leading order) in the Hamiltonian, allowing for arbitrary rotation of the background star. Our model reveals a series of universal relations from first-principles arguments. Besides the well-known relations, we show that the three-wave (next-to-leading-order) interaction coefficients are fully determined by the properties of the linear tide. Therefore, they do not probe new physics of the NS. Nonetheless, not including the three-wave nonlinear tides can lead to significant systematic errors in the gravitational waveform. We support this claim via a hybrid approach that simultaneously captures mode resonances expected in Newtonian hydrodynamics and is consistent with relativistic calculations in the low-frequency expansion. The nonlinear tide in a single NS can cause a phase shift of around 1.7 radians accumulated up to merger compared to the linear tide model; for a binary of similar masses, the phase shift is approximately doubled. Our calculation extends to four-wave interactions, which, for a slowly spinning NS, provide only small corrections and are subdominant compared to the tidal back-reaction on the orbit. For a rapidly rotating NS, the nonlinear centrifugal drive of the f-mode and the four-wave anharmonicity provides a window to study the adiabatic exponent related to internal buoyancy that cannot be probed by the linear and three-wave tides in slowly spinning systems. The anharmonicity cannot lead to resonance locking of the f-mode.

</details>


### [134] [Wave and particle probes of a regular T-duality-inspired black hole with gravitational self-energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07955)
*Alexey Dubinsky*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种受T-对偶性启发的非局域点质量涂抹（引入有限零长尺度）所生成的非奇异黑洞几何。该黑洞的ADM质量包含有限的自能贡献，其极端普朗克尺度残余扇区被提出作为暗物质候选。作者研究了该时空对两种物理信号的影响：大质量标量场的准正则振荡以及近水平粒子/光运动。结果显示：标量场质量增加导致振荡频率升高、阻尼减弱（长寿命振荡）；零长尺度增加使光子轨道和ISCO更紧凑、阴影更小、光子环频率更高、轨道结合能增大。这些结果表明同一修正在波传播、黑洞阴影和圆轨道物理中留下相关印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非局域T-对偶性启发的点质量涂抹（引入零长尺度）所产生的正则化黑洞时空的物理可观测效应，检验其作为暗物质候选的可行性。

Method: 理论分析：研究该正则化黑洞几何中标量场的准正则模（质量依赖性）以及测试粒子和光子的轨道动力学（光子轨道、ISCO、阴影、结合能）。

Result: （1）标量场：质量增大→频率增大、阻尼减弱，产生长寿命振荡；（2）粒子/光：零长尺度增大→光子轨道和ISCO更紧凑、阴影变小、光子环频率增大、轨道结合能增大。

Conclusion: 同一正则化修正（零长尺度）在波传播、黑洞阴影和圆轨道物理中留下相互关联的可观测印记，为检验该模型提供多通道探针。

Abstract: Recently it was shown that a non-local T-duality-inspired smearing of the point mass introduces a finite zero-point length, while the regularized Newtonian gravitational self-energy is promoted to an additional source for the spacetime. The result is a nonsingular black-hole geometry whose ADM mass contains a finite self-energy contribution and whose extremal Planck-scale remnant sector has been proposed as a possible dark-matter component. We study how this spacetime would affect two familiar physical signals: the ringing of a massive scalar field and the motion of particles and light near the horizon. The zero-point length smooths the central region and changes the strong-field potential outside the horizon. On the wave side, we find that making the scalar field heavier increases the oscillation frequency and makes the damping weaker, a behavior associated with long-lived ringing. On the particle side, increasing the zero-point length makes the photon orbit and the innermost stable circular orbit more compact in physical mass units. The corresponding shadow becomes smaller, the photon-ring frequency becomes larger, and the orbital binding energy increases. These results show that the same regularizing correction leaves related imprints in wave propagation, black-hole shadows and circular-orbit physics.

</details>


### [135] [Acceleration in 3D Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08030)
*Cendikiawan Suryaatmadja,Dong-han Yeom,Robert Mann*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在2+1维Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力中发现了一类新的精确解，推广了C-metric。这些度规等于C-metric乘以一个依赖于单一变量的因子，该变量控制时空结构。在Einstein引力中有三类度规，但每类中我们发现了六个不同的子类。讨论基本结构后，我们专注于一个局部AdS的子类。在零耦合极限下，该子类不仅保持良好定义并恢复C-metric，还包含了AdS时空的两种先前未知的表示。此外，我们建立了畴壁的存在性并描绘了其能量条件。还发现了非恒定曲率的新解类，其解释仍有待理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在2+1维Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力中推广C-metric精确解，探索更高曲率引力下的新时空结构，特别是AdS时空的新表示和畴壁等拓扑缺陷的存在性。

Method: 通过引入一个与无质量标量场耦合的乘法因子来推广C-metric，将该因子设为单一变量的函数，在Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力框架下求解场方程，得到六种不同子类解。

Result: 在Einstein引力的三类度规结构基础上，每类中发现了六个不同的子类解。其中一类子解在零耦合极限下不仅恢复C-metric，还发现了AdS时空的两种新表示。同时证明了畴壁的存在性并分析了其能量条件，以及发现了非恒定曲率的新解类。

Conclusion: Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力在2+1维中提供了比Einstein引力更丰富的解结构，包括已知解的推广、AdS时空的新表示以及畴壁等结构。非恒定曲率解类为进一步研究提供了方向。

Abstract: We present a new class of exact solutions in Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity in 2+1 dimensions that generalize the C-metric. This set of metrics equals the C-metric multiplied by a factor which, along with the massless scalar field, depends on a single variable whose value governs the structure of the spacetime. As in Einstein gravity there are three classes of metrics, but within each class we find six distinct subclasses of solutions. After discussing their basic structure, we concentrate only on one subclass that is locally AdS. In the zero-coupling limit, this subclass of solutions not only remains well defined and recovers the C-metric but also encompasses two previously unknown representations of the AdS spacetime. Furthermore, we establish the existence of a domain wall and delineate its energy conditions. We also find new classes of solutions of non-constant curvature, whose interpretation remains to be understood.

</details>


### [136] [An analytic model for a total process of gravitational collapse: From star to Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08088)
*Sinya Aoki,Jorge Ovalle*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出了一个精确解析模型，完整描述了从无视界初始条件到黑洞形成的引力坍缩全过程，追踪了视界从微观形成到宏观稳定的整个演化。模型揭示了两个主要阶段：动力视界增长和裸奇点解析，最终通过视界增长保护了弱宇宙监督假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑洞形成理论多依赖数值模拟或近似分析，缺乏从初始无奇点/无视界状态到黑洞形成的完整解析描述。作者旨在建立一个精确可解的解析模型，以揭示视界动力学形成机制以及奇点与视界的因果关系。

Method: 采用精确解析建模方法，构造了从无视界初始条件到黑洞形成的完整引力坍缩模型。通过追踪视界函数H(t)的演化，得到两个解析阶段：视界在临界时刻tb形成并增长至最终尺寸h=2M；同时分析中心处Ricci曲率奇点的行为。

Result: 成功展示了：（1）表观视界H(t)在有限时间tb内动力学形成，并增长至稳定值；（2）中心r=0处出现可积的Ricci曲率奇点（R^μ_ν∼r^{-2}），但该奇点被增长中的视界因果屏蔽，无需引入奇异物质即可保持弱宇宙监督假设成立。

Conclusion: 该模型为研究量子-经典过渡（H(t)∼ℓ_Planck）提供了理论框架，或许有助于理解黑洞视界形成的量子引力本质以及引力坍缩中的奇点问题。

Abstract: We present an exact analytical model describing a complete gravitational collapse of matter from horizonless initial conditions to black hole formation, tracing the full evolution of the horizon $H(t)$ from its formation at microscopic scales to macroscopic stabilization. The solution reveals two main stages: (i) a dynamical horizon growth, where an apparent horizon $H(t)$ emerges at a critical time $t_b$ until reaching its final size $h=2 M$, demonstrating how trapped surfaces form dynamically in finite time, and (ii) a {naked-}singularity resolution, where an integrable Ricci curvature singularity ($R^μ_ν\sim r^{-2}$) develops at $r=0$, but remains causally hidden by the horizon growth, preserving weak cosmic censorship without exotic matter. The model could offer a framework to study the quantum-to-classical transition ($H(t) \sim \ell_{\rm Planck}$).

</details>


### [137] [Field Theoretic Aspects of Gravity From Direct Action to Quantum Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08134)
*Ghanashyam Date*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文追踪了从经典直接作用（超距作用）到量子场论（场作为自主实体）的概念演变，特别关注引力在量子框架中引入的新特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以JVN研究的“超距作用”观点和马赫原理为锚点，探讨从经典场论到量子场论的概念转变，理清场作为中介物到自主物理实体的演进逻辑。

Method: 通过比较经典框架下的直接作用观与场论观，过渡到量子框架，勾勒量子场论的发展脉络，并纳入引力带来的新特征。采用概念追踪而非系统性综述的方式。

Result: 揭示了在量子框架中，场从力的中介者升格为自主的物理实体；引力在量子场论中引入了不同于其他相互作用的新特征。

Conclusion: 直接作用观与场论观在经典和量子框架下存在根本性差异；量子场论中场的本体论地位发生了质变，而引力为其带来了独特的概念挑战。

Abstract: One of the technical anchors of JVN's research was the "action-at-a-distance" view of gravitational interaction, driven by "Mach's Principle" addressing the origin of inertia. This view exorcises the concept of a field as a mediator of force between separated "particles". However, within a quantum framework, the concept of field as a mediator transcends to field as an autonomous physical entity. Beginning with a comparison between direct action view and field theoretic view in classical framework and moving over to quantum framework, I sketch the strides made in quantum field theory including the new features brought in by gravity. This is intended as a conceptual trace rather than a review.

</details>


### [138] [Chaplygin and Polytropic Kantowski--Sachs Solutions in Teleparallel $F(T)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08343)
*Alexandre Landry*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在teleparallel F(T)引力框架下，利用coframe-spin-connection形式和Coley-Landry不变量方法，发展了Kantowski-Sachs几何的协变重建形式，其中物质由Chaplygin气体(p=-A/ρ^α)和多方气体(p=Kρ^Γ)驱动。通过守恒定律确定流体密度与KS体积的关系，再将其代入场方程对称部分直接重建F(T)函数形式。幂律和指数两种假设产生不同重建分支，在幂律分支中Chaplygin流体产生常数加幂次源项，多方分支产生由多方指数控制的密度幂次；指数分支中自然重建变量为平移不变量X=T0-T，产生平移de Sitter分支。重建模型可解释为局域各向异性宇宙学区段，收缩角向尺度因子对应KS黑洞内部重建分支。通过FT>0和FTT>0评估可生存性，完全扰动稳定性分析留待后续。该重建完全由非线性物质守恒定律驱动，颠倒了标准重建策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在teleparallel F(T)引力中，标准重建策略通常是先预设拉格朗日量再求解物质演化，本文旨在反转这一流程：由非线性物质状态方程（广义Chaplygin气体和多方流体）的守恒定律直接驱动F(T)函数形式的重建，以探索各向异性KS几何中物质与几何之间的耦合关系，并检验重建模型在局域宇宙学及黑洞内部场景下的可行性。

Method: 采用coframe-spin-connection形式和Coley-Landry不变量方法构建KS几何的协变描述；物质由Chaplygin气体和多方流体的非线性状态方程建模；通过守恒定律导出流体密度与KS体积V=A2A3^2的函数关系；将源项标度代入teleparallel场方程对称部分，直接求解F(T)的函数形式；分别采用幂律和指数两种假设分析不同重建分支；利用FT>0和FTT>0评估模型在leading-order的生存性。

Result: 幂律分支：Chaplygin流体产生常数加幂次混合源项；多方流体产生由多方指数控制的密度幂次。指数分支：自然重建变量为X=T0-T，产生平移teleparallel de Sitter分支。模型可解释为局域各向异性宇宙学区域，在角向尺度因子收缩时可解释为KS黑洞内部重建分支。通过FT>0和FTT>0条件可在leading-order评估可行性。

Conclusion: 本文成功实现了由非线性物质守恒定律驱动的teleparallel F(T)引力在KS几何中的重建，颠倒了传统重建策略。重建模型在局域宇宙学和黑洞内部场景均有解释，但分析是局域且分支依赖的，完整扰动稳定性分析留待后续工作。

Abstract: A covariant reconstruction framework for Kantowski--Sachs (KS) geometries sourced by Chaplygin-type and polytropic fluids in teleparallel $F(T)$ gravity is developed using the coframe--spin-connection formalism and the invariant Coley--Landry approach. The matter sector is modeled by nonlinear equations of state, including the generalized Chaplygin gas $p=-A/ρ^α$ and a polytropic law $p=Kρ^Γ$. The corresponding conservation laws determine the dependence of the fluid density on the anisotropic KS volume $V=A_2A_3^2$. These source scalings are then inserted into the symmetric part of the covariant teleparallel field equations and used to reconstruct the functional form of $F(T)$ directly from the KS dynamics. Power-law and exponential ansätze generate distinct invariant reconstruction branches. In the power-law sector, the Chaplygin fluid produces mixed constant-plus-power source terms, while the polytropic sector generates density powers controlled by the polytropic index. In the exponential sector, the natural reconstruction variable is the shifted invariant $X=T_0-T$, leading to shifted teleparallel de Sitter branches. The reconstructed models are interpreted as local anisotropic cosmological sectors and, for contracting angular KS scale factors, as local Kantowski--Sachs black-hole-interior reconstruction branches. The analysis is local and branch-dependent; leading-order viability is assessed through \(F_T>0\) and \(F_{TT}>0\), while a complete perturbative stability analysis is left for future work. The reconstruction is entirely driven by nonlinear matter conservation laws, thereby reversing the standard reconstruction strategy in which the gravitational Lagrangian is prescribed a priori.

</details>


### [139] [Spectroscopic structure of Non-Hermitian $\mathcal{PT}$-Symmetric Klein--Gordon Fields in a Magnetized Cosmic String Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08148)
*Omar Mustafa,Abdullah Guvendi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了磁化宇宙弦时空中非厄米$\mathcal{PT}$对称Klein-Gordon场，通过引入复非最小标量相互作用，构建了有效$\mathcal{PT}$对称Klein-Gordon振子，并利用双合流Heun级数-多项式方法条件精确求解径向方程。研究发现，非厄米$\mathcal{PT}$对称化会引入能量上限，与厄米情况形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索弯曲时空背景下非厄米$\mathcal{PT}$对称相对论量子场的可解析处理框架，并揭示$\mathcal{PT}$对称性在调节允许能谱中的作用。

Method: 采用复非最小标量相互作用$\mathcal{F}_\mu=(0,\mathcal{F}_r,0,0)$，其中$\mathcal{F}_r=\omega r+ib\in\mathbb{C}$，结合磁荷$q=ie$，将系统转化为有效$\mathcal{PT}$对称Klein-Gordon振子（含复库仑和线性相互作用）。径向方程通过双合流Heun级数-多项式方法条件精确求解；在$\omega=0$极限下利用合流超几何级数/多项式精确求解。

Result: 非厄米$\mathcal{PT}$对称化引入了允许能量的上限，而对应的厄米情况则无此限制。通过映射$e=-i\tilde e \Rightarrow q\in \mathbb R$和$b=-i\tilde b \Rightarrow \mathcal F_r\in \mathbb R$可对比厄米对应情形。

Conclusion: 为非厄米$\mathcal{PT}$对称相对论量子场在弯曲时空中的研究提供了可解析处理的框架，证明了$\mathcal{PT}$对称性在调节能谱中的关键作用。

Abstract: We investigate non-Hermitian $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric Klein--Gordon (KG) fields in a magnetized cosmic string spacetime. A complex non-minimal scalar interaction, $\mathcal{F}_μ=(0,\mathcal{F}_{r},0,0)$ with $\mathcal{F}_{r}=ωr+ib\in\mathbb{C}$, indulged with a magnetic charge $q=ie$, is shown to introduce an effective $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric Klein--Gordon oscillator supplemented by complex Coulombic and linear interactions. The resulting radial equation is shown to be conditionally exactly solvable using a biconfluent Heun series-to-polynomial approach. To observe non-Hermitian \(\mathcal{PT}\) symmetrization, we compare with the Hermitian counterpart by mapping $e=-i\tilde e \Rightarrow q\in \mathbb R$ and $b=-i\tilde b \Rightarrow \mathcal F_r\in \mathbb R$. In the limiting case $ω=0$, the system is shown to reduce to an exactly solvable non-Hermitian \(\mathcal{PT}\) symmetric Coulomb-type KG equation using confluent hypergeometric series/polynomials. Hereby, for both \(\mathcal{PT}\) symmetric models considered, we show that non-Hermitian \(\mathcal{PT}\) symmetrization introduces an upper limit for the allowed energies, unlike the corresponding Hermitian cases. These results provide an analytically tractable framework for exploring non-Hermitian relativistic quantum fields in curved spacetimes and demonstrate the role of $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetry in regulating the allowed spectrum.

</details>


### [140] [Static regular black holes in Horndeski theories: analytic no-go and nonanalytic obstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08228)
*Antonio De Felice,Shinji Tsujikawa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Horndeski理论中规则黑洞的存在性条件，证明在一般情况下（非退化移位对称理论），标量场必须为常数且度规退化为Schwarzschild解，从而导致中心奇点。只有在特殊的非解析边界情况（如标量-高斯-博内耦合链）下才可能避免这一结论，但即便如此，中心奇异性仍然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Horndeski理论中能否存在同时具有稳定视界和规则中心（无奇点）的黑洞解，并系统分类各种可能情形下的理论限制。

Method: 通过分析静态球对称渐近平直时空中的标量场方程，分别在视界分支（X非零）和规则分支（X=0）上研究解的解析性条件。利用泰勒展开和退耦条件，结合传播速度发散性、鬼/梯度不稳定性等物理约束进行排除分析。

Result: 视界分支通常被发散传播速度或不稳定性排除；规则分支在非退化移位对称理论中导致非微扰无毛定理（标量常数、度规Schwarzschild、中心奇点）；非移位对称正幂耦合中类似排除适用于与Schwarzschild连续连接的微扰分支；唯一例外是标量-高斯-博内耦合链这一边际非解析完备化，但对应的有毛黑洞仍具有中心奇点。

Conclusion: Horndeski理论中无法同时实现稳定视界和规则中心，所有非平凡标量场配置的黑洞解最终都会产生中心奇异性，即使通过特殊非解析耦合规避了当前无毛定理的限制。

Abstract: Regular black holes in Horndeski theories must have stable horizons and regular centers. We study static, spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat configurations with a time-independent scalar. The horizon branch on which the scalar kinetic term $X$ remains nonzero is generically obstructed by divergent propagation speeds or ghost/gradient instabilities, aside from special degeneracies. On the regular branch, where $X$ vanishes at the horizon, analyticity at the relevant $X=0$ endpoints reduces the leading scalar equation to finite sets of Taylor coefficients. For nondegenerate shift-symmetric theories this gives a nonperturbative current no-hair theorem: the scalar is constant and the metric is Schwarzschild, hence centrally singular for nonzero ADM mass. For non-shift-symmetric positive-power couplings, the corresponding exclusion applies to the perturbative branch continuously connected to Schwarzschild. We also classify marginal nonanalytic departures: covariant regularity fixes the scalar-Gauss-Bonnet chain as the unique marginal nonanalytic completion. Hairy black holes in this completion evade the analytic current step but remain centrally singular.

</details>


### [141] [Quasi-bound states and late-time evolution of a massive fermion around a Reissner-Nordström black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08258)
*Guang-Shang Chen,Cheng-Bo Yang,Shou-Shan Bao,Yue-Liang Wu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带电黑洞周围有质量费米子的准束缚态，通过改进的匹配方案得到了更精确的能谱和衰变宽度，并分析了不同晚期时间区域的振荡幂律行为与指数抑制特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建引力体系中带电费米子束缚态的量子力学类比，改进弱耦合下准束缚态能谱的解析精度，并澄清长时间晚期信号的不同衰减机制。

Method: 将径向方程化为耦合矩阵系统，构造满足内边界（视界）入波条件与外边界无穷远衰减条件的格林函数；在弱耦合|qQ|~mM<1下采用矩阵匹配方案得到准束缚态谱的精细结构修正与衰变宽度；对极端Reissner-Nordström情形单独处理并验证非极端谱的平滑极限；通过时域格林函数支切割线贡献分析晚期的振荡幂律与指数抑制行为，并用直接时域模拟验证。

Result: 得到了包含精细结构修正的准束缚态谱解析表达式与更精确的衰变宽度；极端情形是光滑极限；中间晚期（1/m<t<1/m³M²）呈现振荡幂律行为；极晚期（t>1/m³M²）准束缚态激活产生t^{-5/6}exp(-ηt^{1/3})抑制伴随啁啾相位，最终趋于t^{-5/6}尾。

Conclusion: 准束缚态贡献与幂律尾在时域中共存，不同时间区域由不同机制主导，完善的解析框架为理解引力系统中量子束缚态弛豫提供了理论基础。

Abstract: A massive fermion around a charged black hole provides a gravitational analogue of atomic bound states and their relaxation. In this work, we study this system by formulating the radial equation as a coupled matrix system and constructing the Green's function with ingoing boundary conditions at the horizon and decaying boundary conditions at infinity. In the weak-coupling scenario $|qQ|\sim mM<1$, a matrix matching scheme gives an improved analytic expression of quasi-bound-state spectrum, including fine-structure corrections and more accurate decay widths. The extremal Reissner-Nordström case ($|Q|=M$) is treated separately and shown to be the smooth limiting result of the non-extremal spectrum. We further analyze the branch-cut contribution to the time-domain Green's function in the late-time limit. We confirm an oscillatory power-law behavior in intermediate late-time regime $1/m < t < 1/m^3M^2$. In the far late-time regime $t>1/m^3M^2$, the activation of the quasi-bound states produces an $t^{-5/6}\exp(-ηt^{1/3})$ suppression with a chirping phase before the asymptotic $t^{-5/6}$ tail previously found in the limit $t\to\infty$. Direct time-domain simulations support this distinction and show how the quasi-bound contribution coexists with the familiar power-law component.

</details>


### [142] [5d Schwarzschild-Tangherlini spacetime: MST-like formalism for a Reduced Confluent Heun Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08280)
*Donato Bini,Giorgio Di Russo,Veronica Fantini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维Schwarzschild-Tangherlini解的测地线结构和无质量标量微扰，发展了MST形式主义的新扩展，并通过后牛顿展开和Seiberg-Witten形式主义验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高维黑洞（五维Schwarzschild-Tangherlini解）的动力学性质，包括测地线散射、准正规模式以及微扰理论，旨在发展适用于高维时空的分析工具。

Method: 采用探针极限，计算非束缚测地线的散射角并给出后牛顿和后闵可夫斯基展开（用超几何函数重求和技术）；推导临界圆轨道的Lyapunov指数；对无质量标量场微扰，将径向方程化为约化合流Heun方程，发展MST形式主义的原始扩展；利用量子Seiberg-Witten形式主义独立确定重整化角动量参数ν；计算圆轨道的能流通量至2.5PN阶。

Result: 成功将散射角级数重求和为超几何函数形式；Lyapunov指数用于准正规模式的eikonal估计；发展的MST扩展得到的重整化角动量参数ν与Seiberg-Witten形式主义结果一致；能流通量计算至2.5PN精度。

Conclusion: 在五维Schwarzschild-Tangherlini时空中，所发展的解析方法（MST形式主义扩展、超几何重求和等）有效且自洽，为高维黑洞微扰理论提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study the five-dimensional Schwarzschild-Tangherlini solution, with particular attention to its geodesic structure and massless scalar perturbations. In the probe limit, we present two applications. First, we compute the scattering angle for unbound geodesics showing both post-Newtonian and post-Minkowskian type expansions, and succeeding in resumming the resulting series in terms of hypergeometric functions. Second, we derive the Lyapunov exponent for deviations from a critical circular orbit, which is relevant to the eikonal estimation of quasinormal modes.
  We then investigate the dynamics of massless scalar $(s=0)$ perturbations, for which the radial equation becomes a Reduced Confluent Heun equation. In this $d=5$ Schwarzschild-Tangherlini case we develop an original extension of the standard Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi (MST) formalism and validate the construction by computing the renormalized angular-momentum parameter $ν$, whose value agrees with an independent determination based on the quantum Seiberg-Witten formalism. Finally, we analyze the energy flux from circular orbits, obtaining post-Newtonian results through 2.5PN order.

</details>


### [143] [Gravito-Electromagnetic perturbation of higher derivative corrected Kerr-Newman black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08333)
*Yuanyuan Ji,Zheng-wen Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了缓慢旋转的4阶导数修正Kerr-Newman黑洞的线性引力-电磁扰动，推导了耦合径向主方程并扩展了渐近迭代方法（AIM）以处理耦合二阶ODE，计算了准正规频率（QNFs），发现由c₆, c₇, c₈诱导的宇称简并破缺在高电荷黑洞中尤为显著，并通过解析拟合对QNFs进行了参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解高阶导数修正对旋转带电黑洞线性扰动的影响，特别是宇称简并性（isospectrality）的破坏机制，并发展适用于耦合ODE系统的数值方法以计算准正规频率。

Method: 采用微扰方法，在角动量、高阶导数参数c_i和黑洞电荷的修正阶次下，导出耦合的径向主方程；将渐近迭代方法（AIM）扩展至耦合二阶常微分方程组；利用该方法计算系统的准正规频率。

Result: 发现了轴向和极向扰动扇区之间显著的宇称简并破缺，该破缺由c₆, c₇, c₈参数诱导，且在高电荷黑洞中更为明显。

Conclusion: 高阶导数修正会破坏Kerr-Newman黑洞扰动的宇称简并性，且这种效应随电荷增大而增强；所提出的解析拟合方法可提高QNFs的实用价值。

Abstract: We study linear gravito-electromagnetic perturbations of a slowly rotating 4-derivative corrected Kerr-Newman (KN) black hole (BH), using a perturbative approach beyond the KN geometry up to first order in angular momentum, first order in the higher-derivative parameters ci, i = 1,2...8, and 14th order in the BH electric charge. We derive the corrected coupled radial master equations for both axial and polar perturbation sectors. We extend the asymptotic iteration method (AIM) to apply to coupled second-order ordinary differential equations (ODEs) and employ it to compute the quasinormal frequencies (QNFs) of the system.Our results show pronounced isospectrality breakings between the two parity sectors, induced by c6,c7 and c8,particularly for high charge BH.Finally, we parameterize the QNFs via a analytical fitting procedure for broader applicability.

</details>


### [144] [Signal-to-Noise Ratio Contours for LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08445)
*Kai Schmitz,Joseph D. Romano*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了LISA自相关测量中任意信号强度下期望最优信噪比(SNR)的广义表达式，修正了文献中仅适用于弱信号极限的常用SNR公式，并给出了LISA灵敏度曲线的上界约束（SNR_max约10^4），数值结果已在Zenodo公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中常用的LISA随机引力波背景信噪比表达式仅在弱信号极限下成立，导致大量报道的SNR值不准确，需要推导任意信号强度下有效的广义SNR表达式。

Method: 首次推导了LISA自相关测量中任意信号强度下的期望最优SNR解析表达式，基于广义表达式分析了SNR上界，并给出了不同SNR水平下的广义幂律积分（PLI）灵敏度曲线。

Result: LISA观测时间T_obs在频率范围[f_min, f_max]内不会产生超过SNR_max = sqrt(T_obs(f_max-f_min))的信噪比，对于典型任务参数SNR_max约10^4；LISA SNR等高线存在上界，近似为LISA应变噪声曲线乘以欧拉数e。

Conclusion: 对于不弱于噪声水平约e倍的GWB信号，必须使用新推导的广义SNR表达式进行评估，否者文献中大量基于弱信号极限的SNR值存在误差。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will search for a stochastic gravitational-wave (GW) background at millihertz frequencies, from both astrophysical and cosmological sources, and thereby open a new chapter in GW astronomy. In the literature, LISA's sensitivity to prospective GW background (GWB) signals is often quantified in terms of an expected signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) assuming perfect knowledge of the detector noise. The commonly employed expression for the SNR is, however, valid only in the limit of a weak GWB signal, which renders a large number of SNR values reported in the literature inaccurate. In this paper, we address this issue by deriving for the first time an expression for the expected optimal SNR of a LISA auto-correlation measurement that is valid at arbitrary signal strength. Based on our generalized expression, we conclude that LISA data worth an observing time of T_obs across the frequency band from f_min to f_max will never yield an SNR in excess of SNR_max = sqrt(T_obs(f_max-f_min)), which evaluates to SNR_max <~ 10^4 for typical mission parameters. We illustrate our findings in terms of generalized power-law-integrated (PLI) sensitivity curves at different SNR levels, i.e., LISA SNR contour lines in plots of the GW energy-density power spectrum. In contrast to earlier work on PLI sensitivity curves, we notably find that the LISA SNR contours are bounded from above, approximately by the LISA strain noise curve multiplied by a factor of Euler's number e. For GWB signals not much weaker than this range, the expected SNR for a LISA auto-correlation measurement needs to be evaluated based on our new expression. Our numerical results for the LISA SNR contours are available on Zenodo [https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21275527].

</details>


### [145] [Search for strong lensing of gravitational waves in the binary black hole events from O1-O4a](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08466)
*Ankur Barsode,Koustav N. Maity,Parameswaran Ajith*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对LIGO、Virgo和KAGRA（LVK）在O1-O4a运行期间观测到的引力波事件（包括LVK官方目录及外部组织IAS、OGC发现的额外事件）进行了强引力透镜效应搜索。使用Posterior Overlap 2.0贝叶斯模型选择管道，结合背景和前景模拟评估统计显著性。未发现强透镜证据，所有候选对的透镜概率均低于0.6%，在90%置信水平上限定透镜占比为1.4%。预测在O4、IR1和O5运行阶段实现3σ探测的概率分别约为20%、23%和67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波可能被前景星系或星系团强透镜化，产生多个副本信号。鉴别这些透镜化事件对于正确解释引力波统计特性、提高源定位精度以及研究宇宙学（如哈勃常数测量）具有重要意义。此前搜索存在局限性，需要通过更完善的管道和更全面的事件目录进行系统性搜索。

Method: 基于Posterior Overlap 2.0贝叶斯模型选择管道，对LVK O1-O4a运行期内的双黑洞并合事件（包括LVK官方、IAS和OGC目录中的事件）进行成对透镜候选搜索。通过真实的背景和前景模拟评估管道的鲁棒性和检测效率，定义了新的指标在个体和总体层面评估透镜候选的统计显著性。

Result: 所有候选对的透镜概率均小于0.6%，未发现强透镜存在的证据。在90%置信水平上给出透镜占比上限为1.4%。前九大透镜候选对中有五对来自非LVK目录。预测O4、IR1和O5运行阶段实现3σ探测的概率分别约为20%、23%和67%。

Conclusion: 当前数据中不存在可靠的强透镜引力波事件，透镜占比上限为1.4%。搜索非LVK目录中的事件对提升透镜候选识别有重要意义。随着未来观测运行（特别是O5）数据量的增加，检测到透镜化引力波的可能性将显著提高。

Abstract: A small fraction of the gravitational waves (GWs) currently observable by LIGO, Virgo, and KAGRA (LVK) may be strongly lensed by intervening galaxies and galaxy clusters, potentially producing multiple copies of the same signal. We search for lensed pairs of binary black hole signals detected during the O1-O4a observing runs. We include the events identified by the LVK Collaboration, as well as additional events found by external groups (IAS and OGC). Our search is based on Posterior Overlap 2.0, a fast and efficient Bayesian model-selection pipeline to identify lensed candidates. The search is supplemented by realistic background and foreground simulations to characterize the robustness and detection efficiency of the pipeline, as well as the statistical significance of lensed candidates. We define new metrics to assess the statistical significance of lensing both at the individual and population levels. Our work addresses some of the limitations of previous searches.
