With the probability of lensing $<0.6\%$ for all pairs, we find no evidence for strong lensing in the data and consequently place a $90\%$ upper bound on the lensing fraction of $1.4\%$. With five out of the top nine lensed candidate pairs being from non-LVK catalogs, we also highlight the importance of searching among events reported by multiple GW catalogs. We forecast that the probabilities of making a $3σ$ detection in the fourth (O4), intermediate (IR1), and fifth (O5) observing runs are $\sim 20\%,\, 23\%$, and $67\%$, respectively.

</details>


### [146] [Horizon-redshift transfer in black-hole direct-wave damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08570)
*Wen-Biao Han,Ye Jiang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过计算黑洞并合中直接波的Teukolsky响应，证明其包络阻尼γ_eff并非直接测量视界表面重力κ，而是受Kerr屏蔽和源卷积影响的有限窗口传递可观测量。对GW250114的分析给出γ_eff/κ≈0.6，与QNM减除残差一致。瞬时源恢复κ附近阻尼，而有限持续源实现均得到γ_eff<κ。GW231226残差检验支持该有限窗口阻尼预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决黑洞并合直接波包络是否以Kerr表面重力率衰减的疑问，探究直接波阻尼与视界动力学的关系。

Method: 计算红移拉伸的近视界源驱动的复频自旋-2、ℓ=m=2 Teukolsky响应；通过Kerr屏蔽和源卷积将局域表面重力尺度κ映射为无穷远处测量的有限窗口包络阻尼γ_eff；采用瞬时源、有限持续期源、试验粒子和径向归一化源等多种实现进行比较。

Result: GW250114: γ_eff/κ≈0.6, γ_eff≈0.4 ms⁻¹, 与QNM减除残差及H1-L1联合残差分析一致；瞬时源恢复κ附近阻尼；有限持续源实现均得到γ_eff<κ；GW231226残差检验支持有限窗口阻尼预测。

Conclusion: 直接波包络阻尼是视界红移传递可观测量，而非表面重力的直接测量。

Abstract: Direct waves from black-hole mergers may probe horizon dynamics, but their observed envelopes need not decay at the Kerr surface-gravity rate. We compute the complex-frequency spin-$-2$, $\ell=m=2$ Teukolsky response driven by a redshift-stretched near-horizon source. Through Kerr screening and source convolution, the calculation maps the local surface-gravity scale $κ$ into the finite-window envelope damping $γ_{\rm eff}$ measured at infinity. For GW250114, this calculation gives $γ_{\rm eff}/κ\simeq0.6$, or $γ_{\rm eff}\simeq0.4~{\rm ms}^{-1}$, consistent with QNM-subtracted residuals and a joint H1--L1 residual analysis. An instantaneous-source control recovers the impulse-response damping near $κ$, whereas finite-duration plunge-source, test-particle and radial-normalized source realizations give $γ_{\rm eff}<κ$. A residual-level check in GW231226 favours the same finite-window damping prediction. These results identify direct-wave envelope damping as a horizon-redshift transfer observable rather than a direct measurement of surface gravity.

</details>


### [147] [Beyond black hole spectroscopy: Quasinormal mode contamination by massless scalars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08619)
*Miguel Yulo Asuncion,Giovanni D'Addario,Thomas P. Sotiriou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究修正引力理论（Horndeski标量-张量理论）中黑洞铃宕（ringdown）信号的特征，指出除了传统的Kerr拟正则模频率偏移外，还会存在新场（标量场）的拟正则模频率污染。研究发现，在移位对称Horndeski作用量下，只有标量-高斯-邦尼特耦合项同时贡献两种效应，且两者出现在同一微扰阶；若放松对标量振幅被电荷比q抑制的假设，污染效应可在更低阶出现并主导频率偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检验广义相对论（GR）的黑洞铃宕方法主要关注Kerr度规拟正则模（QNM）频率的偏移，但最近研究表明，在超出GR的场景中，新场（如标量场）与引力非最小耦合时，铃宕信号也会被新场的QNM频率污染。本文旨在系统研究移位对称Horndeski作用量下黑洞扰动的特征，明确频率偏移与污染效应出现的条件和相对重要性。

Method: 作者在移位对称Horndeski作用量框架下，对无质量标量场与引力的所有导致二阶运动方程的相互作用进行线性微扰分析。采用标量电荷与黑洞质量比q的微扰展开，假设标量振幅被q抑制（到q²阶），研究发现只有标量-高斯-邦尼特耦合项同时贡献频率偏移和污染效应，且两者出现在同一阶。若放松振幅抑制假设，污染可在q的领头阶出现。

Result: 到q²阶，标量-高斯-邦尼特（Gauss-Bonnet）耦合是唯一同时贡献频率偏移和污染效应的项；频率偏移和污染出现在同一微扰阶；若放松标量振幅被q抑制的假设，污染效应可在q的领头阶出现，从而主导频率偏移；此时污染还接收来自额外耦合的次领头阶修正。

Conclusion: 检验GR时不能仅关注频率偏移，还需考虑新场QNM污染效应；两种效应可在同一阶出现；污染可能比预期的更显著，需重新评估当前铃宕检验GR的策略。

Abstract: Testing General Relativity (GR) with black hole ringdowns has conventionally focused on attempting to detect shifts away from the quasinormal mode (QNM) frequencies of the Kerr metric. It has recently been argued, however, that the ringdown signal will also be contaminated with the QNM frequencies of any new fields that are present in a beyond-GR scenario, provided that they couple nonminimally to gravity. We study black hole perturbations for the shift-symmetric Horndeski action, which includes all interactions between a massless scalar and gravity that lead to second order equations upon variation. We perturb linearly in the field and also employ a perturbative expansion in the scalar charge per unit black hole mass, $q$. Assuming that the scalar amplitude is suppressed by $q$, we demonstrate that, to order $q^2$, the coupling between the scalar and the Gauss-Bonnet invariant is the only term that contributes to both frequency shifts and contamination, and that the two effects appear at the same perturbative order. If the assumption about the suppression of the scalar amplitude is relaxed, contamination can appear at leading order in $q$, and hence dominate over frequency shifts. In this case, contamination also receives subleading corrections from an additional coupling

</details>


### [148] [Shadows and lensing signatures of a rotating black hole in a Hernquist dark matter halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08650)
*A. A. Araújo Filho,Arun Kumar,N. Heidari,C. F. S. Pereira,Amilcar R. Queiroz,V. B. Bezerra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌入Hernquist暗物质晕的旋转黑洞的光学性质，通过非复化Newman-Janis方法构建时空，推导零测地线方程、有效势和光子轨道，利用阴影和引力透镜效应对暗物质晕参数进行观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗物质晕对黑洞光学观测信号（阴影、引力透镜）的影响，并利用Event Horizon Telescope对Sgr A*和M87*的测量数据以及ESO325-G004的爱因斯坦环观测，约束Hernquist暗物质晕的参数。

Method: 采用非复化Newman-Janis方法从静态Hernquist黑洞生成旋转时空；求解Hamilton-Jacobi方程的可分离性获得临界碰撞参数和阴影轮廓；利用强场和弱场引力透镜分析推导对数偏折角和相对论像位置；结合EHT和ESO325-G004观测数据进行参数拟合。

Result: 旋转参数主要导致阴影偏移和畸变，Hernquist暗物质晕扩增光子捕获区域并增大阴影尺寸。Sgr A*给出最强约束：1σ下无量纲晕参数$\hatρ\sim(2.7-3.8)\times10^{-3}$，2σ下$\hatρ\sim(4.1-5.2)\times10^{-3}$。弱场透镜中暗物质晕已贡献领先偏折角，ESO325-G004给出$\hatρ\lesssim0.00939$（1σ）和$\hatρ\lesssim0.01963$（2σ）。

Conclusion: 暗物质晕显著影响旋转黑洞的光学特征，通过阴影和引力透镜观测可以有效约束暗物质晕参数，为检验暗物质模型提供新的天体物理探针。

Abstract: We investigate the optical properties of a rotating black hole immersed in a Hernquist dark matter halo. The spacetime is generated from a static Hernquist black hole through the noncomplexification version of the Newman-Janis procedure, yielding a Kerr-like geometry whose halo contribution is encoded in the radial function $Δ(r)$ \cite{AraujoFilho:2026hernquist}. We derive the null geodesic equations, effective potentials, radial acceleration, and representative three-dimensional photon trajectories around the event horizon and ergoregion. Using the separability of the Hamilton-Jacobi equation, we obtain the critical impact parameters of unstable spherical photon orbits and construct the shadow contours for a distant observer. The rotation parameter mainly shifts and distorts the shadow, whereas the Hernquist halo enlarges the photon capture region and increases the apparent shadow size. Comparing the area-equivalent shadow diameter with the Event Horizon Telescope measurements of Sgr A$^\ast$ and M87$^\ast$, we constrain the dimensionless halo parameter $\hatρ=M^2ρ$. The strongest restriction comes from Sgr A$^\ast$, giving $\hatρ\sim(2.7-3.8)\times10^{-3}$ at $1σ$ and $\hatρ\sim(4.1-5.2)\times10^{-3}$ at $2σ$. We also analyze strong- and weak-field gravitational lensing. In the strong-field regime, the halo shifts the unstable photon orbit and critical impact parameter, controlling the logarithmic deflection angle and the position of relativistic images. In the weak-field regime, the halo contributes already to the leading bending angle and enhances deviations from Kerr as $ρ$ grows. From the Einstein ring of ESO325-G004, we further obtain $0\leq\hatρ\lesssim0.00939$ at $1σ$ and $0\leq\hatρ\lesssim0.01963$ at $2σ$.

</details>

### [149] [Chiral Tube Algebras I: Topological Defect Lines, Twisted Modules, and Finite Gauging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07786)
*Nathan Benjamin,Ho Tat Lam,Conghuan Luo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入手性管代数（chiral tube algebras），统一并推广了二维共形场论中的手性代数与拓扑缺陷线（TDLs）两种对称性概念。手性管代数将手性代数的作用从局域希尔伯特空间扩展到由TDL扭动的缺陷希尔伯特空间，并允许由TDL连接的非局域手性流，从而在不同缺陷空间之间建立映射。该框架为有限规范变换下手性代数的像提供了自然描述，并通过Kac-Moody代数、W代数、超共形代数及其orbifold/bosonization等例子加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一二维共形场论中手性代数与拓扑缺陷线这两种互补的对称性概念，并扩展手性代数的适用范围，使其能作用于缺陷希尔伯特空间，同时为有限规范变换下手性代数的行为提供统一描述框架。

Method: 引入手性管代数，该代数推广手性代数：(1) 将作用扩展到TDL扭动的缺陷希尔伯特空间；(2) 允许由TDL连接的非局域手性流，在不同缺陷空间之间映射。通过Kac-Moody、W代数、超共形代数及其orbifold/bosonization等例子进行具体构造。

Result: 建立了手性管代数的框架，构造了其不可约模（同构于对应手性代数的扭模），并用于组织局域和缺陷希尔伯特空间。

Conclusion: 手性管代数统一了手性代数与TDLs，为有限规范变换下手性代数的像提供了自然描述。后续工作将研究具有分数自旋的非局域手性流生成的手性管代数，这是传统手性代数所不具备的。

Abstract: Chiral algebras and topological defect lines (TDLs) represent two complementary notions of symmetry in 2d conformal field theories. In this paper, we introduce chiral tube algebras to unify and extend these two notions. Chiral tube algebras generalize chiral algebras in two ways. First, they extend the action of chiral algebras beyond the local Hilbert space to include defect Hilbert spaces twisted by TDLs. Second, they allow for non-local chiral currents attached by TDLs and thus can map between different defect Hilbert spaces, analogous to the tube algebras of TDLs. Since local chiral currents can become non-local after finite gauging, chiral tube algebras provide a natural framework for describing the image of chiral algebras under such gauging. We illustrate this framework through a variety of examples that generalize familiar chiral algebras, including Kac-Moody algebras, $\mathcal{W}$ algebras, superconformal algebras, and their orbifolds/bosonizations. We construct their irreducible modules, which are isomorphic to twisted modules of the corresponding chiral algebras, and use them to organize local and defect Hilbert spaces. In a subsequent paper, we will study chiral tube algebras generated by non-local chiral currents with fractional spins, which have no counterparts in chiral algebras.

</details>


### [150] [Quantization of Gravity on Null Hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07785)
*Luca Ciambelli,Marc S. Klinger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了广义相对论特征初值问题(CIVP)的算子代数量子化方案。通过分别量子化两个相交的零超曲面，并利用角对称性（超助推、超旋转、超平移）在共享的余二维角处粘合，得到了一个引力子区域的在壳代数。超平移被量子化为量子信道，通过Stinespring扩张定理纳入代数；超助推和超旋转则实现了爱因斯坦方程的引力约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管ADM形式论（类空超曲面上的Cauchy问题）广为人知，但零超曲面上的特征初值问题(CIVP)在量子引力研究中相对未被充分探索。CIVP编码了丰富物理，尤其是两个零超曲面在其共享角处的粘合，为研究引力子区域量子代数提供了自然框架。

Method: (1) 分别量子化每个零超曲面，以角对称性（超助推、超旋转、超平移）为组织原则；(2) 在初始联合截面上将二者粘合，构造联合算子代数；(3) 超平移被量子化为量子信道（非代数保持映射），通过广义Stinespring扩张定理纳入代数结构；(4) 超助推和超旋转实现爱因斯坦方程在量子代数层面的引力约束。

Result: 构建了一个包含完整角对称性（超助推、超旋转、超平移）内作用的算子代数，其中超助推和超旋转对应面积和Hájiček一形式的量子化，超平移对应沿两个零方向膨胀的量子化并被实现为量子信道。该代数在CIVP局部无焦散的有效域内，成为由一对零超曲面界定的引力子区域的候选在壳代数。

Conclusion: 该工作为CIVP的量子化提供了算子代数框架，揭示了角对称性在零超曲面量子化中的核心作用，并通过量子信道机制处理了规范约束，为量子引力中子区域代数的构造提供了新路径。

Abstract: The initial value problem for general relativity on spacelike hypersurfaces is famously captured by the ADM formalism. Less well known is the Cauchy problem for general relativity on null hypersurfaces, which goes under the name of the Characteristic Initial Value Problem (CIVP). The CIVP is formulated on a pair of intersecting null hypersurfaces, encoding rich physics in the interplay between their respective initial data, and especially the gluing of these hypersurfaces at their shared codimension-two corner. In this work, we construct an operator-algebraic quantization of the CIVP. To do so, we first quantize each null hypersurface separately, using the corner symmetries as a guiding principle, and then glue them together at the joint initial cut. The resulting algebra admits an inner action of the full corner symmetry (semi)group consisting of superboosts, superrotations, and supertranslations. These are associated, respectively, with the quantization of the area, the Hájiček one-form, and the expansion along both null directions. Supertranslations do not act as algebra preserving maps, but instead are quantized to quantum channels and included into the algebra via a generalized form of the Stinespring dilation theorem. The inclusion of superboosts and superrotations implements the gravitational constraints arising from Einstein's equations at the level of the quantum algebra. By virtue of the CIVP, in its local caustic-free domain of validity, our construction yields a candidate for the on-shell algebra of a gravitational subregion subtended by a pair of null hypersurfaces.

</details>


### [151] [Phase-dependent kink collisions and dual critical-velocity branches in the complex sine-Gordon model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08752)
*Mohammad Mohammadi,Farnaz Eizadbaksh,Vahideh Bagheri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文通过数值模拟研究了复正弦-戈登（CSG）模型中复扭结-扭结碰撞动力学，揭示了初始速度与相对相位对碰撞结果的联合影响，发现了两个不同的临界速度分支，展示了从散射到捕获、长寿命双孤子态、呼吸子态及辐射剖面发射的丰富动力学行为，表明相对相位作为有效的内部自由度在复杂孤子碰撞中起重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSG模型包含内部相位自由度，这显著改变了其孤立波解的动力学行为，而此前研究尚不充分，特别是关于初始速度与相对相位如何共同决定碰撞结果。

Method: 对CSG模型中复扭结-扭结碰撞进行数值模拟，系统扫描初始速度和相对相位参数空间，并计算辐射剖面的能量、能量密度极值、动能和梯度贡献以及碰撞中心场模量等诊断量。

Result: 发现了两个不同的临界速度分支：一个分支中增加初始速度促进捕获，另一个分支中则恢复散射。碰撞结果包括散射、捕获、长寿命双孤子（bion）形成、呼吸子态和辐射发射。各诊断量在临界点处呈现尖锐转变。

Conclusion: 相对相位作为有效的内部自由度，在复杂孤子碰撞动力学中发挥着重要的调控作用，这显著区别于实正弦-戈登模型的弹性碰撞行为。

Abstract: The complex sine-Gordon (CSG) model contains an internal phase degree of freedom that strongly modifies the dynamics of its solitary-wave solutions. We present a numerical study of complex kink--kink collisions and determine how the final state depends jointly on the initial velocity and relative phase. In contrast with the elastic collisions of the real sine-Gordon model, the CSG system exhibits scattering, capture, long-lived bion formation, breather-like states, and emission of radiative profiles. The simulations reveal two distinct phase-dependent branches of critical velocity. In one branch, increasing the initial velocity promotes capture, whereas in the other it restores scattering. This dual structure highlights the rich velocity--phase dependence of the collision dynamics. We also compute the energy carried by radiative profiles and examine extreme values of the energy density, kinetic and gradient contributions, and field modulus at the collision center. These quantities show sharp transitions at critical points and provide sensitive diagnostics of phase-controlled dynamics. These results suggest that the relative phase behaves as an effective internal degree of freedom that plays an important role in the collision dynamics of complex solitons.

</details>


### [152] [No off-diagonal quantum focusing for Rényi divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07799)
*Tanay Kibe,Pratik Roy*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明任何满足数据处理、张量可加性和经典-量子条件匹配的Rényi型散度都无法扩展为完整的Rényi聚焦猜想，否定了量子聚焦猜想的Rényi推广可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子聚焦猜想是半经典引力普适吸引性的数学表达，其非对角部分涉及相对熵的双零形状变分的单调性。近期Rényi量子零能量条件的证明引发了关于是否存在完整Rényi聚焦陈述的疑问，本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 通过分析Rényi型散度所需满足的数据处理不等式、张量可加性和经典-量子条件匹配等性质，构建反例或论证来检验完整Rényi聚焦猜想是否成立。

Result: 对于任何满足数据处理、张量可加性和经典-量子条件匹配的Rényi型散度，完整的Rényi聚焦猜想不成立。

Conclusion: 否定了量子聚焦猜想的Rényi推广可能性，为理解半经典引力中熵与几何的关系提供了重要约束。

Abstract: The quantum focusing conjecture is a mathematical expression of the idea that semiclassical gravity remains universally attractive. Its off-diagonal part is a monotonicity condition on the double null shape variation of relative entropy on distinct null generators, and has been argued to follow from strong subadditivity of entanglement entropy. Recent proof of a diagonal Rényi quantum null energy condition raises the question: does a full Rényi focusing statement also hold? We answer this question negatively for any Rényi-type divergence satisfying data processing, tensor additivity, and matched classical--quantum conditioning.

</details>


### [153] [Relative entropy for $λφ^4$ in the Rindler wedge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07810)
*Markus B. Fröb,Albert Much,Kyriakos Papadopoulos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文研究了相互作用λφ⁴理论中Rindler楔形区域真空态与相干态之间的相对熵，在一阶λ微扰下构造了相互作用的Weyl代数，利用Tomita-Takesaki模理论和Araki-Uhlmann公式计算相对熵，验证了其约化为相互作用的boost Noether荷，并确认了Bekenstein界的成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自由场理论中相对熵与经典Noether荷之间的对应关系推广到相互作用场论，检验Bekenstein界在相互作用理论中的有效性。

Method: 在一阶λ微扰下构造相互作用λφ⁴理论的Weyl代数，利用Tomita-Takesaki模理论和Araki-Uhlmann公式计算Rindler楔形区域中真空态与相干态之间的相对熵。

Result: 证明了在相互作用理论中，相对熵仍约化为经典的（相互作用的）boost Noether荷，且Bekenstein界在微扰框架下成立。

Conclusion: 自由场中相对熵与Noether荷的对应关系在相互作用λφ⁴理论中仍然成立，进一步支持了Bekenstein界的普适性。

Abstract: We consider the relative entropy between the vacuum and a coherent state in the Rindler wedge for an interacting $λφ^4$ theory to first order in $λ$. We construct the perturbatively interacting Weyl algebra of the wedge, and employ Tomita--Takesaki modular theory and the Araki--Uhlmann formula to compute the relative entropy. We verify that the relative entropy reduces to the classical (interacting) boost Noether charge, analogously to the free theory, and that the Bekenstein bound holds.

</details>


### [154] [The Energy-Momentum-News Complex near Future Null Infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07872)
*Jelle Hartong,Emil Have,Vijay Nenmeli,Gerben Oling*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究三维和四维广义相对论中渐近平坦真空解，专注于近零无穷远处的几何结构。通过Carroll协变的Bondi-Sachs规范，从最一般的Carroll度量数据出发构造渐近解，并定义边界能量-动量-新闻复量，由此导出Carroll协变的Bondi损失方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解渐近平坦时空在近零无穷远处的几何结构，并建立Carroll几何框架下规范不变的边界能动张量与新闻张量之间的Ward恒等式，从而推广传统的Bondi损失方程。

Method: 采用Carroll协变的Bondi-Sachs规范构造近零无穷远处的渐近解；通过对截断面上的Einstein-Hilbert作用量添加边界项进行重整化，变分得到边界能量-动量-新闻复量；分析边界微分同胚、Weyl变换和Carroll boosts三种残差对称性对应的Ward恒等式。

Result: 得到了边界能量-动量-新闻复量满足的三个Ward型恒等式：前两个由边界微分同胚和Weyl规范不变性给出，第三个（Carroll boost对应的恒等式）具有反常，其反常项来自重整化作用量的变分。这些恒等式共同导出Carroll协变的Bondi损失方程。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Carroll几何框架下近零无穷远处渐近解的系统构造方法，并通过边界作用量的变分原理统一了边界能动张量、新闻张量及其对应的守恒律，为引力辐射和渐近对称性的研究提供了更简洁的几何描述。

Abstract: We study asymptotically flat vacuum solutions of general relativity in three and four dimensions, with an emphasis on the geometric structures that emerge near null infinity. We construct asymptotic solutions to the three- and four-dimensional Einstein equations near future null infinity, which is a conformal Carroll manifold, starting from the most general Carroll metric data allowed by the Einstein equations. We use a Carroll-covariant version of Bondi--Sachs gauge, whose residual transformations act on the boundary Carroll geometry and shear as boundary diffeomorphisms, Weyl transformations and Carroll boosts. We then define a boundary energy-momentum-news complex at future null infinity by varying a suitably renormalised action with respect to the boundary Carroll metric data and shear. This involves adding boundary terms to the Einstein--Hilbert action on a cut-off surface near future null infinity. The boundary energy-momentum-news complex obeys two relations due to the boundary diffeomorphism and Weyl gauge invariance of the renormalised action. A third relation, due to the Carroll boost, is anomalous, and the corresponding anomaly is obtained from the variation of the renormalised action. Together, these Ward-type identities obeyed by the boundary energy-momentum-news complex lead to a Carroll-covariant generalisation of the Bondi loss equations.

</details>


### [155] [2-Group global symmetry in the compactified M2-brane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07924)
*F. Caro-Pérez,M. P Garcia del Moral,A. Restuccia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究带有四形式通量背景下的玻色M2膜广义全局对称性，发现其单极子和缠绕对称扇区由于Wess-Zumino耦合而形成非平凡的2-群全局对称性，而非简单的直积结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索M2膜在非平凡四形式通量背景下的广义全局对称性结构，特别是理解Wess-Zumino耦合如何影响对称扇区的组合方式，以及这种结构与靶空间通量量子化之间的关系。

Method: 以$AdS_5\times S^2_{1} \times S^2_{2}\times T^2$紧化为例，分析单极子和缠绕对称扇区，引入混合背景-规范结构，通过Postnikov类刻画靶空间量子化通量。

Result: 发现对称扇区并非普通直积，而是形成2-群全局对称性；识别了混合背景-规范结构；证明靶空间量子化通量决定关联的Postnikov类。

Conclusion: M2膜世界体积理论实现高群对称性，诱导世界体积上的通量量子化，并将全局对称性结构与M理论中的量子化通量联系起来。

Abstract: We study generalized global symmetries of the bosonic M2-brane in eleven-dimensional backgrounds with non-trivial four-form flux. Focusing on compactifications of the form $AdS_5\times S^2_{1} \times S^2_{2}\times T^2$, we show that the monopole and winding symmetry sectors of the membrane do not organize as an ordinary direct product once the Wess--Zumino coupling is included. Instead, they combine into a non-trivial 2-group global symmetry. We identify the corresponding mixed background-gauge structure and show that the quantized target space flux determines the associated Postnikov class. This provides a concrete realization of higher-group symmetry in the worldvolume theory of the M2-brane induces a flux quantization on the worldvolume and relates its global symmetry structure to the introduction of a quantized flux in M-theory.

</details>


### [156] [Scalar Charges in a Self-Dual Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07938)
*Seth Grable,Jamison Barcelona*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在恒定自对偶磁场中精确求解了3+1维标量QED模型的配分函数、β函数和传播子，首次给出了自对偶背景下物质场传播子的闭式有限表达式，为研究平行电磁场中的带电粒子及手征磁效应等提供了玩具模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在自对偶磁场背景中精确求解标量QED，获得闭式解析结果，填补自对偶背景下物质场传播子闭式表达的空白，并为平行电磁场中的带电粒子物理、手征磁效应及脉冲星物理提供理论工具。

Method: 采用朗道能级基矢，在恒定自对偶磁场构型中，对3+1维标量QED模型进行精确闭式解析计算，得到了配分函数、β函数及传播子的闭式有限表达式。

Result: 首次给出了自对偶磁场背景中物质场传播子的闭式有限表达式，并得到了配分函数和β函数的精确解析结果。

Conclusion: 该模型可作为平行电磁场中带电粒子的玩具模型，并能自然推广到3+1维手征磁效应及脉冲星物理的研究中。

Abstract: In this work, we present a \(3+1d\) scalar QED model in a constant self-dual magnetic field configuration. We provide exact closed form analytic calculations of the partition function, the \(β\)-function, and the propagators. To our knowledge, we present the first closed-form finite expression for the matter field propagators in a self-dual background, which is made accessible by working entirely in the Landau level basis. This theory serves as a toy model for charged particles in parallel electric and magnetic fields, with natural extensions to studies of \(3+1d\) chiral magnetic effects and pulsar physics.

</details>


### [157] [More on Majorana fields, modular localization and near saturation of the Tsirelson bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07949)
*J. G. A. Caribé,J. R. A. Quispe,S. P. Sorella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用1+1维自由无质量Majorana场研究量子场论中的Bell-CHSH不等式，通过模楔局域化方法展示了Tsirelson边界的近饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论中Bell不等式的违反程度及其与Tsirelson边界的关系是量子基础研究的重要问题，作者希望通过模楔局域化这一数学工具精确刻画这一违反程度。

Method: 采用1+1维自由无质量Majorana场作为理论模型，运用模楔局域化（modular wedge localization）技术来分析Bell-CHSH不等式的违反行为。

Result: 成功展示了Bell-CHSH不等式在量子场论中能够近饱和Tsirelson边界，即达到量子力学允许的最大违反程度。

Conclusion: 模楔局域化是研究量子场论中非局域性的有效工具，自由Majorana场模型为理解量子场论中Bell不等式违反的极限提供了清晰的理论框架。

Abstract: The free massless Majorana field in 1+1 dimensions is employed to study the Bell-CHSH inequality in Quantum Field Theory. Use of the modular wedge localization enables us to show the near saturation of the Tsirelson bound.

</details>


### [158] [Ghosts Hunting in the Yang-Mills Vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07954)
*Seth Grable*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析了3+1维单圈半经典Yang-Mills理论的零模，发现零模由背景场的规范冗余产生。通过恰当规范固定（引入玻色鬼项）和zeta函数正则化，得到了闭合形式的有限单圈有效理论，并重现了著名的单圈Yang-Mills beta函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并处理Yang-Mills理论中半经典展开时出现的零模问题，这些零模由背景场的规范冗余产生，如果不恰当处理会导致红外发散或计算不一致。

Method: 在3+1维中对单圈半经典Yang-Mills理论进行零模分析；通过引入玻色鬼项进行恰当的规范固定；采用zeta函数正则化处理发散；最终得到闭合形式的有限单圈有效理论。

Result: 成功得到闭合形式的有限单圈有效理论，并重现了Yang-Mills理论的单圈beta函数。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了通过恰当规范固定和正则化方法可以有效处理半经典Yang-Mills理论中的零模问题，所得结果与已知的Yang-Mills重整化群流一致，验证了方法的正确性。

Abstract: In this work I analyze the zero-modes of a one-loop semi-classical Yang-Mills theory in \(3+1d\). I find the zero-modes are generated by gauge redundancy of the background field. With proper gauge fixing, via the introduction of a bosonic ghost term, and the use of zeta-function regularization, a finite one-loop effective theory is calculated in closed form, reproducing the well known one-loop Yang-Mills beta-function.

</details>


### [159] [Carroll supergravities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08329)
*Marc Henneaux*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文明确推导了D=4时空维度中N=1超引力的电性和磁性Carroll极限，该方法具有普适性，可推广至扩展超引力模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超引力在Carroll极限下的行为，为非相对论极限下的超对称引力理论提供理论基础。

Method: 采用电性和磁性两种Carroll极限方法，对D=4维N=1超引力进行明确推导，并指出该方法的通用性。

Result: 成功推导出N=1超引力的电性和磁性Carroll极限，并证明该方法可推广到扩展超引力。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解超引力在超速极限（Carroll极限）下的行为提供了系统框架，具有扩展到更复杂超引力模型的潜力。

Abstract: The electric and magnetic carrollian limits of $N=1$ supergravity in $D=4$ spacetime dimensions are explicitly derived. The approach is general and applies also to extended supergravity models

</details>


### [160] [Gauge-Invariant Off-Shell Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08426)
*Kang-Sin Choi,Hyeseon Im*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种规范不变且重整化的离壳质量函数，通过将 pinch 技术推广到任意长的费米子线，使自能和顶点函数成为规范不变且过程无关的，并证明其离壳时等价于费曼规范自能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自能在离壳时依赖于规范选取，这导致质量函数缺乏规范不变性，无法与 Schwinger-Dyson 方程和格点 QCD 的离壳质量函数直接比较。

Method: 将 pinch 技术推广到任意长费米子线，利用 Ward-Takahashi 恒等式在每个顶点局域作用，抵消规范依赖部分，得到费曼规范自能。

Result: 得到的离壳自能是规范不变的，在壳重整化后质量函数等于极点处的物理质量；该自能还给出红外有限的标量质量函数，可直接与 Schwinger-Dyson 和格点结果比较；该抵消在内部费米子线上是局域的，完全离壳成立。

Conclusion: 该方法在任意多外光子的振幅中均适用，通过计算最小离壳 Compton 振幅进行了显式验证，为定义规范不变的离壳质量函数提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We define a gauge-invariant and renormalized off-shell mass function in quantum field theory. The conventional self-energy is gauge-dependent off-shell. We generalize the self-energy, together with the corresponding vertex function, to be gauge-invariant and process-independent. To do this, we extend the pinch technique to an arbitrarily long fermion line. The Ward-Takahashi identity acting locally at each vertex cancels the gauge-dependent piece exactly, leaving the Feynman-gauge self-energy. On-shell renormalization then yields a mass function that is gauge-invariant, scheme-independent and equal to the physical mass at the pole; the same self-energy also yields an infrared-finite scalar mass function directly comparable with Schwinger-Dyson and lattice determinations. The cancellation is local to the internal fermion line and holds entirely off-shell, so the self-energy is well-defined on each internal segment of an amplitude with arbitrarily many external photons; we demonstrate this explicitly by computing the minimal off-shell Compton amplitude.

</details>


### [161] [Gram--Wishart--Stiefel formulation of the $N=2$, large--$d$ gauge theory in 1D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08481)
*Badis Ydri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了BFSS/BMN矩阵量子力学在格点上的N=2大d平面端点理论的Gram/Wishart/Stiefel形式化，通过重新组织端点自由度为秩二Wishart特征值和相对Stiefel角变量，分析了holonomy不变量A、B和R²。核心发现是holonomy势的大R对齐渐近行为中，线性项-A被吸收进高斯部分产生偏移质量参数，纯-A理论在Bessel/HCIZ结构下精确可解。B型展开的有限多项式截断导致的大d微扰性边界是截断伪像，求和局部横向完备化后恢复具有物理偏移质量的Wishart鞍点，连续极限重现D_Λ通道的通用-2d贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展BFSS/BMN矩阵量子力学格点理论中端点自由度的Gram/Wishart/Stiefel形式化，以更清晰地分析holonomy不变量与对齐渐近行为，并解决B型展开中有限截断导致的伪微扰边界问题。

Method: 采用Gram/Wishart/Stiefel变量重新参数化端点自由度，将秩二Wishart特征值与Stiefel角变量分离；通过分析holonomy势的大R对齐渐近行为，吸收线性-A项到高斯部分；利用纯-A理论的精确可解性确定Bessel/HCIZ结构；处理B型展开的横向完备化与补偿项求和。

Result: 得到偏移质量参数(α_Λ)_eff=α_Λ-1/2；精确O(2)角积分编码在秩二Bessel核中；纯-A理论固定了Bessel/HCIZ指数结构与前置因子；B型展开有限截断的大d微扰性边界被证明是伪像；完整求和后恢复具有物理偏移质量的Wishart鞍点；连续极限重现D_Λ通道的-2d贡献，而β_Λ通道超出纯横向B型描述范围。

Conclusion: Gram/Wishart/Stiefel形式化为理解BFSS/BMN矩阵量子力学端点理论的holonomy结构提供了有力工具，澄清了偏移质量参数的来源，揭示了截断伪像的本质，并界定了纯横向B型描述的有效范围——仅能捕获D_Λ通道而无法描述β_Λ通道。

Abstract: We develop in this paper the Gram/Wishart/Stiefel formulation of the \(N=2\), large--\(d\) planar endpoint theory of the BFSS/BMN matrix quantum mechanics on the lattice, obtained in our previous work. In this formulation, the endpoint degrees of freedom are reorganized into rank--two Wishart eigenvalues and relative Stiefel angular variables. This allows the holonomy invariants \(A\), \(B\), and \(R^2=A^2+B^2\) to be analyzed directly in terms of radial and angular Gram data.
  A central point is the large-\(R\) aligned asymptotics of the holonomy potential. Its universal linear contribution \(-A\) is absorbed into the Gaussian sector, producing the shifted mass parameter
  \((α_Λ)_{\rm eff}=α_Λ-1/2\). In the Gram/Wishart/Stiefel variables, the exact \(O(2)\) angular integral encodes this shifted sector in a rank--two Bessel kernel. The pure \(-A\) theory, which is exactly solvable in Cartesian variables, then fixes the leading Bessel/HCIZ structure: its exponential part selects the aligned configuration, while its prefactor removes the spurious doubled Wishart entropy.
  We then apply this structure to the transverse \(B\)-type expansion and its non-polynomial toy completion. Finite polynomial truncations lead to an apparent large--\(d\) perturbativity bound incompatible with the continuum limit, but this bound is shown to be an artifact of truncation. After summing the local transverse completion and balancing the compensating \(+A\) term, the Wishart saddle is recovered with the physical shifted mass. The resulting continuum behavior reproduces the universal \(-2d\) contribution of the \(D_Λ\)-channel, while the genuinely anisotropic \(β_Λ\)-channel lies outside the scope of a pure transverse \(B\)-type description.

</details>


### [162] [Quantum corrections to the near-extremal thermodynamics of (warped) BTZ black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08482)
*Emilie Despontin,Stéphane Detournay,Robinson Mancilla,Chiara Toldo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究BTZ和翘曲BTZ黑洞近极端热力学中的单圈效应，重点关注极端喉部中零模本征模的演化。在三维拓扑质量引力框架下，通过Newman-Penrose形式，比较了BTZ的近视界与全几何本征值问题，明确了Schwarzian模和转动模在低温下的行为，并将相同策略推广到翘曲BTZ。发现转动模对两类黑洞的近极端处理不可或缺，且不能在不指定边界条件的情况下被丢弃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解近极端黑洞热力学中零模的量子效应，特别是转动模在BTZ和翘曲BTZ黑洞中的作用，以及边界条件对模截断的影响。

Method: 使用三维拓扑质量引力（含引力Chern-Simons项）理论框架，结合Newman-Penrose形式，分别进行近视界近似和全几何本征值问题分析，并比较两种方法的结果。

Result: 明确了BTZ黑洞中Schwarzian模和转动模在低温下的激发行为；将分析成功推广到翘曲BTZ；发现转动模对一致描述近极端热力学至关重要。

Conclusion: 转动模是近极端黑洞处理中的关键组成部分，其保留或丢弃必须依据具体的边界条件，否则可能导致不一致的热力学描述。

Abstract: We study one-loop effects in the near-extremal thermodynamics of BTZ and warped BTZ black holes, with particular emphasis on the fate of eigenmodes that become zero modes in the extremal throat. Our analysis is formulated in three-dimensional Topologically Massive Gravity, a higher derivative theory characterized by the presence of a gravitational Chern--Simons term, and it makes use of the Newman--Penrose formulation. For BTZ, we compare the near-horizon computation with the full-geometry eigenvalue problem and identify how the Schwarzian and rotational sectors are lifted at small temperature. We then extend the same strategy to warped BTZ. We find that rotational modes are essential for a consistent near-extremal treatment of both BTZ and warped BTZ black holes, and cannot be discarded without first specifying the boundary conditions.

</details>


### [163] [The Remnant of an Evaporating Rotating Regular Black Hole from the Generalized Entropy in the Final Stage of Evaporation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08661)
*Shingo Takeuchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过将旋转规则黑洞的质量表示为极端极限质量加上参数α，基于广义熵非负性及熵中面积项与修正项符号不变的假设，确定了修正项为零时的α下界α₁，该有限值意味着黑洞质量不能小于m_ext+α₁，从而在蒸发末期形成残余物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旋转规则黑洞蒸发末期的广义熵，探索残余物形成的可能性，同时考虑黑洞旋转的普遍性以及中心区域精细结构在蒸发末期的重要性。

Method: 将黑洞质量写作m_ext+α，基于广义熵非负性和面积项与修正项符号在蒸发全程不变的假设，通过令修正项为零来确定α的下界α₁。

Result: 修正项为零时对应的α₁为有限值，表明黑洞质量存在下界m_ext+α₁，即蒸发末期会形成残余物。

Conclusion: 旋转规则黑洞在蒸发末期因广义熵约束而无法完全蒸发，会残留一个质量有限的残余物。

Abstract: We express the generalized entropy (GE) of the Hawking radiation in the final stage of an evaporating rotating regular black hole (BH) by writing the mass of the BH as $m_{\rm ext}+α$, where $m_{\rm ext}$ represents the mass at the extremal limit and $α$ is a parameter. Generally, entropy is non-negative. Based on this fact, we assume that, in the GE considered in this study, the signs of the contributions from the area term and the correction term remain unchanged throughout the entire evaporation process of the BH. Therefore, we regard $α$ at which the correction term vanishes as its lower bound and determine it. As a result, we find that such a value of $α$ is finite. Denoting this value by $α_1$, this result indicates that the mass of the BH cannot become smaller than $m_{\rm ext}+α_1$, which can be interpreted as the emergence of a remnant at the final stage of BH evaporation. The BH considered in this study is a rotating regular BH. The regularization is motivated by the fact that the fine structure of the central region becomes relevant in the final stage of evaporation. A rotating BH is considered from the viewpoint of generality.

</details>


### [164] [Entanglement Wedge Reconstruction without Holographic Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08684)
*Seiji Terashima*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证全息量子纠错（HQECC）解释与纠缠楔重构应分离，指出有限N全息CFT中不存在保护性不可见扇区，超重力场无法实现区域无关的逻辑算符共享，各边界区域应拥有自适应的体解释而非共享逻辑算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清纠缠楔重构与全息量子纠错解释之间的区别，质疑HaPPY型码中区域无关逻辑算符机制在真实全息CFT中的有效性。

Method: 通过定域性论证分析算符对易关系，对比HaPPY型码与有限N全息CFT的代数结构，论证应力张量等低能可观测量的物理支撑与引力修饰对逻辑算符共享的限制。

Result: 有限N全息CFT中不存在保护性不可见扇区，超重力场等低能可观测量能够探测体算符的物理支撑与引力修饰；全局与子区域HKLL公式在N=∞时的吻合是自由场论结果，而非量子纠错的体现。

Conclusion: 纠缠楔重构应独立于全息量子纠错解释，采纳"子区域互补性"方案：每个边界区域拥有自身代码保持的低能代数和区域自适应体解释，而非共享逻辑算符。

Abstract: Bulk reconstruction is a central problem in AdS/CFT, and entanglement wedge reconstruction is its subregion version. We argue that this subregion statement should be separated from the stronger holographic quantum error correction interpretation, in which one region-independent logical bulk operator has code-preserving representatives in several boundary regions. A simple locality argument shows that such a common reconstruction must commute with the code-preserving local algebras in the complementary regions. This is the mechanism realized in HaPPY-type codes: the erased regions are blind to a protected logical algebra. An ordinary finite $N$ holographic CFT does not have such a protected invisible sector for supergravity fields. Its low-energy local observables, in particular, suitably smeared stress tensors, detect the physical support and gravitational dressing of ordinary bulk operators, up to possible center or superselection data. Thus, there is no such holographic quantum error correction and the $N=\infty$ agreement of global and subregion HKLL formulae is a free-theory statement. What remains is entanglement wedge reconstruction without holographic quantum error correction, or subregion complementarity: each boundary region has its own code-preserving low-energy algebra and its own region-adapted bulk interpretation, rather than a shared logical operator.

</details>


### [165] [The mini-Page Curve in Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08737)
*Ricardo Espíndola,Shoichiro Miyashita*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究宇宙学视界（de Sitter空间）中霍金辐射对何时开始携带信息逃逸的问题。通过构造从AdS₂到dS₂的插值流几何，利用交叉积构造将半影代数量提升为Type II_∞因子，计算参考态与霍金对态之间的熵差，发现该熵差呈现特征性的迷你Page曲线，其最小值位置对应信息开始逃离宇宙学视界的时间。进一步证明代数熵与纠缠楔截断的广义熵一致，并推导出李雅普诺夫指数λ=2π/β，识别出搅乱时间（scrambling time）为信息可被静态补丁观测者访问的特征时间尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞信息悖论启示了信息从蒸发黑洞逃逸的机制，本文旨在回答宇宙学视界的类似问题：单个霍金对何时开始将信息携带出de Sitter视界。

Method: 在一类从渐近AdS₂边界平滑插值到dS₂静态补丁的二维流几何中，利用局域算符及其模共轭构造探针态模拟霍金对发射，通过交叉积构造将半影代数量提升为Type II_∞因子，计算热场双参考态与霍金对态之间的熵差。

Result: 熵差呈现特征性迷你Page曲线：从接近零开始，在τ≈β/8处达到最小值，随后增加。代数熵与跟踪沿视界发射粒子的纠缠楔截断的广义熵一致。相对模流生成李雅普诺夫指数λ=2π/β。

Conclusion: 熵差最小值位置（τ≈β/8）被解释为量子信息开始逃离宇宙学视界的时间。李雅普诺夫指数λ=2π/β将搅乱时间识别为霍金对所携带信息可被静态补丁观测者访问的特征时标。

Abstract: The black hole information paradox has motivated extensive study of how and when information escapes from evaporating black holes. Here we address the analogous question for cosmological horizons: when does an individual Hawking pair begin to carry information out of a de Sitter horizon? We study this problem in a class of two-dimensional flow geometries that interpolate smoothly between an asymptotic AdS$ _2 $ boundary and a dS$ _2 $ static patch. Modeling the emission of a Hawking pair via a probe state constructed from local operators and their modular conjugates, we promote the centaur algebra of observables to a Type II$ _\infty $ factor through the crossed-product construction. This allows us to compute the entropy difference between the thermofield-double reference state and the Hawking-pair state. We find that this difference traces a characteristic mini-Page curve for the cosmological horizon: it starts near zero, reaches a minimum near $ τ\approx β/8 $ before increasing again. We interpret the location of the minimum as the time at which quantum information begins to escape the cosmological horizon. Extending the analysis to the microcanonical ensemble, we show that the algebraic entropy coincides with the generalized entropy of an entanglement wedge cut that tracks the emitted particle along the horizon. Furthermore, the relative modular flow generated between the two states yields a Lyapunov exponent $λ=2π/β$, identifying the scrambling time as the scale at which the information carried by the pair becomes accessible to a static-patch observer.

</details>


### [166] [Decoupling Limit of Quiver Theories and the Angular Spectra of Extreme C-metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08747)
*Peng Yang,Kilar Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了极端带电C度规的角特征值问题。在极端极限下，控制微分方程从具有五个正则奇点的Fuchs方程退化为汇合扩展Heun方程。通过构建对偶四维𝒩=2, SU(2)×SU(2)线性箭形规范理论中的解耦极限，推导了参数词典和重正化Matone关系来吸收奇点融合引起的宏观留数偏移。基于角方程的规则边界条件，利用瞬子计数方法建立了代数量子化条件，得到了与数值结果一致的角特征值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极端极限下，C度规的角特征值问题从五个正则奇点的Fuchs方程退化为汇合扩展Heun方程，传统的解析方法难以处理此类退化方程，需要发展新的解析框架来求解角谱。

Method: 1) 在极端带电C度规中取Q→M极限，使微分方程退化为汇合扩展Heun方程；2) 构建对偶四维𝒩=2, SU(2)×SU(2)线性箭形规范理论中的解耦极限；3) 推导参数词典和重正化Matone关系，吸收奇点融合引起的宏观留数偏移；4) 基于规则边界条件，利用瞬子计数方法建立代数量子化条件。

Result: 建立了代数量子化条件，得到的角特征值与数值计算结果一致。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于规范理论/引力对偶的解析方法有效解决了极端C度规角特征值问题，为处理更复杂的退化微分方程提供了新的途径。

Abstract: We investigate the angular eigenvalue problem of the extreme charged C-metric. In the extreme limit ($Q \to M$), the governing differential equation degenerates from a Fuchsian equation with five regular singular points into a Confluent Extended Heun Equation. To evaluate the angular spectrum analytically, we formulate a decoupling limit within the dual four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$, $\mathrm{SU(2)}\times \mathrm{SU(2)}$ linear quiver gauge theory. Within this framework, we derive the parameter dictionary and renormalized Matone relations, which absorb the macroscopic residue shifts induced by the singularity fusion. Based on the regular boundary conditions of the angular equation, we utilize the instanton counting method to establish an algebraic quantization condition, yielding angular eigenvalues consistent with numerical results.

</details>


### [167] [Entropy bounds, Geroch process, and the sign of deformation parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04905)
*Bijan Bagchi,Akshat Pandey,Parmest Roy,Sauvik Sen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于将系统从视界附近丢入黑洞的Geroch过程，在广义不确定原理（GUP）框架下研究了形变对Bekenstein熵界的影响，涵盖(3+1)和(2+1)维度。半经典分析表明，负GUP形变使熵界普遍放宽，正形变则使其收紧，这可以理解为近视界红移的普朗克尺度修正的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在广义不确定原理（GUP）背景下，形变参数对Bekenstein熵界的影响，并统一不同维度下的分析框架。

Method: 采用Geroch提出的将系统从黑洞视界附近丢入的过程，在半经典框架下结合GUP进行熵界分析，分别在(3+1)和(2+1)维度下推导。

Result: 负GUP形变导致熵界普遍放宽（松弛），而正GUP形变则使熵界更加严格（收紧）。

Conclusion: GUP形变对Bekenstein熵界的影响具有符号依赖性，可解释为近视界红移受到普朗克尺度修正的结果。

Abstract: Based on Geroch's process of dropping a system into a black hole from the vicinity of the horizon, we investigate in this paper the influence of deformation on the Bekenstein entropy bound both for (3+1) and (2+1) dimensions in the context of a generalized uncertainty principle (GUP). While providing a coherent framework that sets an upper limit on the entropy across dimensions we show, within a semiclassical treatment, that while a negative GUP deformation yields a universal relaxation of the bound, a positive deformation tightens it. Our results may be interpreted as a response to Planck-scale modifications of the near-horizon redshift.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [On the rational cohomology of moduli spaces of Prym curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07825)
*Claudio Fontanari*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带扭的Prym曲线模空间${\overline{Pr}_{g,n}^{\hspace{0.05cm}(m_1, \ldots, m_n)}}$的低次有理上同调群，证明了在奇数度≤3时这些群消失，且2次群是代数的。结果覆盖了经典Prym曲线和具有简单分支的Prym曲线模空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解扭Prym曲线模空间的低次有理上同调群结构，特别是奇数次群的消失性和2次群的代数性，以推广经典Prym曲线模空间的相关结论。

Method: 通过分析整数扭$0\leq m_i\leq 1$且和为偶数的条件，研究模空间${\overline{Pr}_{g,n}^{\hspace{0.05cm}(m_1, \ldots, m_n)}}$的低次有理上同调群，利用代数几何和上同调理论工具进行证明。

Result: 证明了在奇数度≤3时这些有理上同调群消失，且2次群是代数的。

Conclusion: 该结果统一并推广了经典Prym曲线和具有简单分支的Prym曲线模空间的上同调性质。

Abstract: We investigate the low degree rational cohomology groups of the moduli space of twisted Prym curves ${\overline{Pr}_{g,n}^{\hspace{0.05cm}(m_1, \ldots, m_n)}} $, where the integer twists $0\leq m_i\leq 1$ have even sum over $i$. We prove that these groups vanish in odd degree $\leq3$ and that the group in degree $2$ is algebraic. In particular, the results cover the classical moduli spaces of Prym curves and Prym curves with simple ramifications.

</details>


### [169] [Algebraizability of Vector Bundles over Real Algebraic Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07856)
*Hanqi Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用动机同伦理论研究实代数簇上拓扑向量丛代数化问题，证明了维数≤3时Stiefel-Whitney类的代数性充要条件，并发现维数4时存在与Pontryagin类p1和Stiefel-Whitney类w4相关的额外障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究实代数簇上拓扑向量丛在何种条件下可以代数化（即同构于代数向量丛），这是代数几何与拓扑学交叉领域的基础问题。

Method: 采用动机同伦理论（motivic homotopy theory）作为主要分析工具，通过研究特征类的代数性质来判定拓扑向量丛的代数化可能性。

Result: 1) 当维度≤3时，Stiefel-Whitney类的代数性是拓扑向量丛代数化的充要条件；2) 当维度=4且X(R)紧致时，即使特征类都是代数的，仍存在与p1和w4相关的额外代数化障碍；3) 给出了该障碍非平凡的实例，并研究了K0群。

Conclusion: 建立了特征类代数性与向量丛代数化之间的深层联系，揭示了低维情形下代数化的完整刻画，并指出在4维情形存在超出特征类控制范围的深层次障碍。

Abstract: Let $X$ be an affine smooth real algebraic variety (in the sense of Bochnak, Coste, and Roy) and let $V$ be a topological vector bundle over $X(\mathbb{R})$. We investigate the problem of deciding whether $V$ is topologically isomorphic to an algebraic vector bundle using motivic homotopy theory. We prove that if $\dim X\leq 3$, then the algebraicity of Stiefel-Whitney classes is a necessary and sufficient condition for $V$ to be algebraizable. Next, we show that when $\dim X=4$ and $X(\mathbb{R})$ is compact, even if the characteristic classes of $V$ are algebraic, there is still an obstruction to algebraizing $V$ related to the Pontryagin class $p_1$ and the Stiefel-Whitney class $w_4$. Then we give some applications of this result. Namely, we give an example where this obstruction is nontrivial, and we investigate the group $\mathrm{K}_0(X)$.

</details>


### [170] [A $(24_4,32_3)$-configuration on the Schur quartic with logarithmic Chern slope $14/5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07898)
*Bartosz Naskręcki,Piotr Pokora*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在Schur四次曲面$X\subset\mathbb{P}^3$上构造了一个由24条直线构成的连通排列，其奇异点仅为32个普通三重交点，并证明了该排列的$\overline{c}_1^2/\overline{c}_2=14/5$，否定了K3曲面特化下$8/3$的有界性猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验并反驳一个关于有理曲线横截排列的$8/3$上界猜想在K3曲面特化情形下的正确性。

Method: 在Schur四次曲面上显式构造24条直线的连通排列，通过爆破三重交点计算对数陈类$\overline{c}_1^2$和$\overline{c}_2$，利用有限域混合整数搜索辅助发现，但证明不依赖该搜索。

Result: 得到$\overline{c}_1^2(Y,B)=112$，$\overline{c}_2(Y,B)=40$，比值$14/5=2.8$，超过了$8/3\approx2.667$的猜想上界。

Conclusion: 该反例表明K3曲面上横截有理曲线排列的$8/3$上界猜想不成立，同时该构造是Schur四次曲面上48条第二类直线的一半，另一半通过射影自同构得到。

Abstract: Let $X\subset\mathbb{P}^3$ be the Schur quartic \[
  x_0^4-x_0x_1^3-x_2^4+x_2x_3^3=0. \] We exhibit a connected arrangement of $24$ lines on $X$, defined over $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-3})$, whose singular locus consists of $32$ ordinary triple points and no other intersections. Each line contains four triple points. The resulting reduced divisor $D$ satisfies $D\sim6H$, where $H$ is the hyperplane class. If $π:Y\to X$ blows up the triple points and $B=(π^{-1}D)_{\mathrm{red}}$, then \[
  \overline{c}_{1}^{2}(Y,B)=112,\qquad \overline{c}_{2}(Y,B)=40,
  \qquad \frac{\overline{c}_{1}^{2}(Y,B)}{\overline{c}_{2}(Y,B)}=\frac{14}{5}. \] This gives a negative answer to the K3-surface specialization of the proposed $8/3$ bound for transversal arrangements of rational curves. The configuration is one half of the $48$ lines of the second kind on $X$; an explicit projective automorphism exchanges the two halves. We deliver the line parametrizations and all $32$ triple-point coordinates. Ancillary exact-arithmetic data record the $120$ line-containment coefficients and all $276$ pair-incidence determinants. A finite-field mixed-integer search is described only as the discovery procedure and is not used in the proof.

</details>


### [171] [A technique for computing oriented cohomology rings of semisimple algebraic groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07900)
*Raj Gandhi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用形式Demazure算子计算半单代数群G的面向余上同调环h*(G)的有限生成元与关系式的技术，并给出了A₁、A₂、B₂型伴随单连通群的面向余上同调环的最小表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为半单代数群在满足局部化公理的面向余上同调理论中，提供一种系统计算其环结构和生成元关系的通用方法。

Method: 通过形式Demazure算子来构造有限生成元与关系式，并应用于低秩群的具体计算。

Result: 成功给出了A₁、A₂、B₂型伴随群和单连通群的面向余上同调环的最小表示。

Conclusion: 该技术有效且能推广至更高秩群，为代数群余上同调环的结构研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We present a technique for computing a finite set of generators and relations for the ring $\mathrm{h}^*(G)$ in terms of formal Demazure operators, where $\mathrm{h}^*$ is an oriented cohomology theory satisfying the localization axiom and $G$ is a semisimple algebraic group. Using this technique, we give minimal presentations for the oriented cohomology rings of the adjoint and simply-connected groups of types $A_1$, $A_2$, and $B_2$.

</details>


### [172] [Complex curves in o-minimal geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08052)
*Spencer Dembner*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究可定义黎曼曲面的 coherent 上同调与向量丛分类问题，证明非紧曲线的上同调完全集中于尖点处，计算了仿射直线上的结构层上同调并揭示其与丢番图近似的联系，同时刻画了可定义黎曼曲面具有可定义紧化的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 o-极小性在复解析几何中取得了显著进展，但即使在曲线情形，coherent 上同调和向量丛分类几乎仍属未知，填补这一空白是本文的主要动机。

Method: 通过对可定义全纯函数边界行为的精细分析，研究 $\mathbb{R}_{\mathrm{an}}$ 及类似结构中非紧曲线的上同调性质。

Result: 证明了非紧曲线的上同调完全集中于尖点处；计算了仿射直线上结构层的上同调；建立了与丢番图近似的联系；给出了可定义黎曼曲面具有可定义紧化的刻画条件。

Conclusion: 本文推进了 o-极小性与复解析几何交叉领域的理解，为可定义曲线的上同调理论提供了基础性结果，并揭示了其与丢番图逼近的深层联系。

Abstract: There has recently been considerable progress relating o-minimality to complex analytic geometry. Yet almost nothing is known about coherent cohomology or the classification of vector bundles, even for curves. In $\mathbb{R}_{\mathrm{an}}$ and similar structures, we show that cohomology of noncompact curves is concentrated entirely at punctures. As an application, we compute the cohomology of the structure sheaf on the affine line and describe a connection to Diophantine approximation. Finally, we use similar techniques to characterize which definable Riemann surfaces have definable compactifications. The proofs are based on a careful analysis of boundary behavior for definable holomorphic functions.

</details>


### [173] [Tubular Neighbourhoods of Pfaffian Sets and Applications to Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08370)
*Paul Lezeau,Martin Lotz*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文推导了光滑Pfaffian超曲面管状邻域体积的界，推广了代数簇的已知结果，并将其应用于神经网络的鲁棒性分析，得到了带Pfaffian激活函数的分类器条件数的尾部分布界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将代数几何中关于代数簇管状邻域体积的已知结果推广到更一般的Pfaffian超曲面，并利用这些界来分析神经网络分类器的鲁棒性。

Method: 推导了以Pfaffian格式给出的管状邻域体积上界，并将该理论应用于均匀分布和高斯分布下带Pfaffian激活函数的神经网络分类器的条件数概率分布分析。特别地，对具有有理权重的单隐层sigmoid网络，推导了决策边界管状邻域的多项式宽度界。

Result: 得到了Pfaffian超曲面管状邻域体积的广义界，以及神经网络条件数的尾部分布界；对于单隐层sigmoid网络，得到了决策边界管状邻域的多项式宽度界。

Conclusion: 本文为Pfaffian超曲面的几何分析提供了新工具，并为理解神经网络分类器的鲁棒性提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We derive bounds for the volume of tubular neighbourhoods of smooth Pfaffian hypersurfaces, generalising known results for algebraic varieties. The bounds are given in terms of the Pfaffian format of the defining functions. As an application, we obtain tail bounds on the probability distribution of a condition number measuring the robustness of neural network classifiers with Pfaffian activation functions, in both the uniform and Gaussian settings. In the special case of single-hidden-layer sigmoid networks with rational weights, we derive polynomial-in-width bounds for tubular neighbourhoods of the decision boundary.

</details>


### [174] [A counterexample to Purdy's inequality for hyperplane arrangements in projective three-space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08463)
*Mateusz Michałek,Piotr Pokora*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文给出了射影三维空间中超平面排列的Purdy不等式的一个反例。研究发现，除了已知的两条斜线配置障碍外，还存在另一种障碍——G(3,3,4)型单项式反射排列的子排列，其参数使得p-ℓ+n+2 = -1，证明了对Purdy不等式的精细化陈述是错误的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验射影三维空间中超平面排列的Purdy不等式的精细化形式的正确性，探究除了已知的两条斜线配置障碍外是否还存在其他反例。

Method: 通过反射排列搜索，发现了G(3,3,4)型单项式反射排列的一个子排列；从对偶视角分析该配置的交点、交线数量，计算p-ℓ+n+2的值来验证不等式是否成立。

Result: 该反射排列子排列的对偶配置具有n=12, ℓ=58, p=43，计算得p-ℓ+n+2=-1，即不等式不成立。该配置不在两条斜线中，证明其是独立于已知障碍的新反例。

Conclusion: 仅排除两条斜线配置障碍不足以修正Purdy不等式，存在其他类型的反例，精细化陈述是错误的。

Abstract: We record an explicit counterexample to a refined form of Purdy's inequality for essential hyperplane arrangements in projective three-space. Let $\mathcal{A}$ be an arrangement of $n$ hyperplanes in $\mathbb{P}^3_{\mathbb{C}}$. Let $\ell$ be the number of distinct intersection lines of $\mathcal{A}$, and let $p$ be the number of intersection points, where an intersection point means a point at which at least three hyperplanes meet. The expected inequality is \[
  p-\ell+n+2\geq 0. \] The classical obstruction is the rank $2+2$ product arrangement, or dually a configuration of points contained in two skew lines. We explain this obstruction first, and then show that it is not the only one. The reflection-arrangement search leads naturally to a subarrangement of the monomial reflection arrangement of type $G(3,3,4)$. Looking dually, this configuration is not contained in two skew lines, and has \[
  f_0(S)=12,\qquad f_1(S)=58,\qquad f_2(S)=43. \] Therefore its dual arrangement has \[
  n=12,\qquad \ell=58,\qquad p=43, \] and hence \[
  p-\ell+n+2=-1. \] Thus the refined statement excluding only the two-skew-lines obstruction is false.

</details>


### [175] [Torsion-stabilized modular curves of level $p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08564)
*Michael Temkin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文是模曲线X(N)新积分模型𝒳(N)系列的第一篇论文，定义了𝒳(N)为Y(N)在空间\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{1,N^2}中的闭包，证明了当N=p时，𝒳(p)是Katz-Mazur模型在超奇异点处的爆破，从而(𝒳(p),Y(p))是(widetilde{𝒳}(p),Y(p))的最小环面分辨率，且给出了𝒳(p)参数化Γ-等变稳定属1曲线的可平滑化显式条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为模曲线X(N)构建新的积分模型，尤其是处理一般级数N的情况，并为后续第二篇论文奠定基础。

Method: 将𝒳(N)定义为Y(N)在空间\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{1,N^2}中的闭包；针对N=p的情况，证明𝒳(p)是Katz-Mazur模型在超奇异点处的爆破，并给出可平滑化的显式条件。

Result: 对于N=p，𝒳(p)是Katz-Mazur模型在超奇异点处的爆破，(𝒳(p),Y(p))是(widetilde{𝒳}(p),Y(p))的最小环面分辨率，且在(ℤ,ℤ[1/p])上是对数光滑的；给出了𝒳(p)参数化Γ-等变稳定属1曲线的两个显式可平滑化条件。

Conclusion: 本文为一般级数N的情况提供了必要的背景和定义，并完整解决了N=p的情形；完整的一般级数结果将在第二篇论文中给出。

Abstract: This is the first paper of a project on new integral models $\mathcal{X}(N)$ of the modular curve $X(N)$. The final results for a general level $N$ will be obtained in the second paper, while this paper is devoted to giving all necessary background and definitions applicable to any $N$ and then working out the case of $\mathcal{X}(p)$ with all possible details. We define $\mathcal{X}(N)$ as the closure of $Y(N)$ in the space $\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{1,N^2}=\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{1,Γ}$, where $Γ=(\mathbb{Z}/N\mathbb{Z})^2$, and show that for $N=p$ it is the blowup of the Katz-Mazur model $\widetilde{\mathcal{X}}(p)$ at all supersingular points, and hence $(\mathcal{X}(p),Y(p))$ is the minimal toroidal resolution of $(\widetilde{\mathcal{X}}(p),Y(p))$. In fact, it is even log smooth over $(\mathbb{Z},\mathbb{Z}[1/p])$, but this is special for the case when $p=N$. One can tautologically view $\mathcal{X}(p)$ as the moduli space of $Γ$-stabilized genus-1 curves $(E,Γ)$ which can be smoothed to an elliptic curve labelled by its $N$-torsion, but our main results provide explicit criteria of the smoothability: $\mathcal{X}(p)$ parameterizes $Γ$-equivariant stable genus-1 curves $(E,Γ)$ such that the action satisfies two explicit conditions formulated in the paper.

</details>
